﻿namespace = algeria_events

# Conquest of Algeira - Success event
algeria_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:algeria_algiers_state_scope
	title = algeria_events.1.t
	desc = algeria_events.1.d
	flavor = algeria_events.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_french_algeria"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = algeria_je_success
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
			}
			save_scope_as = algeria_algiers_state_scope
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_the_algerian_departements
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_french_foreign_legion
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				is_ruler = no
			}
			save_scope_as = algerian_parade_general_scope
		}
	}

	option = { # Focus on partying with the lads
		name = algeria_events.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_divided_monarchists
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = bonapartist_progress_increase_15_tt
				je:je_divided_monarchists = {
					add_progress = { value = 15 name = divided_monarchists_bonapartist_progress_bar }
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = vanquished_barbary
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = officers
			value = large_radicals
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = soldiers
			value = medium_radicals
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
	}
	option = { # Focus on migration
		name = algeria_events.1.b
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_divided_monarchists
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = legitimist_progress_increase_15_tt
				je:je_divided_monarchists = {
					add_progress = { value = 15 name = divided_monarchists_legitimist_progress_bar }
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = algerian_migration
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = algerian_migration
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = algerian_migration
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
	}
	option = {  # Focus on appeasement
		name = algeria_events.1.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_divided_monarchists
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = orleanist_progress_increase_15_tt
				je:je_divided_monarchists = {
					add_progress = { value = 15 name = divided_monarchists_orleanist_progress_bar }
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:berber
				value = small_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:maghrebi
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:berber
				value = small_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:maghrebi
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:berber
				value = small_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:maghrebi
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			add_claim = c:FRA
		}
	}
}

# Crémieux Decree
algeria_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:cremieux_decree_sign_state_scope
	title = algeria_events.2.t
	desc = algeria_events.2.d
	flavor = algeria_events.2.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_french_algeria"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	trigger = {
		has_variable = algeria_je_success
		NOR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_ethnostate
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_national_supremacy
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
				state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
				state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
			}
			is_being_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_pop = {
				culture = cu:sephardic
				religion = rel:jewish
			}
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			is_in_government = yes
			leader = {
				OR = {
					culture = cu:sephardic
					culture = cu:ashkenazi
					religion = rel:jewish
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_reformer
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_market_liberal
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist
				}
				NOT = { has_trait = bigoted }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
					state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
				}
				is_being_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:sephardic
					religion = rel:jewish
				}
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = cu:sephardic
					religion = rel:jewish
				}
				save_scope_as = cremieux_decree_pop_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = cremieux_decree_state_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = cremieux_decree_sign_state_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
				leader = {
					OR = {
						culture = cu:sephardic
						culture = cu:ashkenazi
						religion = rel:jewish
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_reformer
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_market_liberal
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist
					}
					NOT = { has_trait = bigoted }
				}
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = cremieux_decree_leader_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = cremieux_decree_ig_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
					state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
				}
			}
			add_culture_standard_of_living_modifier = {
				culture = cu:sephardic
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:sephardic
					}
				}
				add_loyalists_in_state = {
					culture = cu:sephardic
					value = 0.20
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:maghrebi
					}
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					culture = cu:maghrebi
					value = 0.05
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:berber
					}
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					culture = cu:berber
					value = 0.05
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.2.b
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
					state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:sephardic
					}
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					culture = cu:sephardic
					value = 0.05
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:maghrebi
					}
				}
				add_loyalists_in_state = {
					culture = cu:maghrebi
					value = 0.02
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:berber
					}
				}
				add_loyalists_in_state = {
					culture = cu:berber
					value = 0.02
				}
			}
		}
	}

algeria_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = algeria_events.3.t
	desc = algeria_events.3.d
	flavor = algeria_events.3.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_sepoy_mutiny"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	trigger = {
		# triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = algerian_fiasco_ig_leader
			}
			save_scope_as = algerian_fiasco_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = the_algerian_fiasco
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = widespread_condemnation_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.02
			culture = cu:french
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.4
			pop_type = officers
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.02
			pop_type = soldiers
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				scope:algerian_fiasco_ig_leader = {
					kill_character = yes
				}
			}
			50 = {
				scope:algerian_fiasco_ig_leader = {
					add_trait = wounded
					add_trait = psychological_affliction
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Algerians Raid Your Shit
algeria_events.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:algerian_raided_state_scope
	title = algeria_events.5.t
	desc = algeria_events.5.d
	flavor = algeria_events.5.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_vandalized_storefront"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:CON
				c:CON ?= { relations:root <= relations_threshold:neutral }
				NOT = {	c:CON ?= { is_subject_of = c:FRA } }
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:AIT
				c:AIT ?= { relations:root <= relations_threshold:neutral }
				NOT = { c:AIT ?= { is_subject_of = c:FRA } }
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:MAS
				c:MAS ?= { relations:root <= relations_threshold:neutral }
				NOT = { c:MAS ?= { is_subject_of = c:FRA } }
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:TUG
				c:TUG ?= { relations:root <= relations_threshold:neutral }
				NOT = { c:TUG ?= { is_subject_of = c:FRA } }
			}
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = general
			is_ruler = no
			is_heir = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = algerian_raided_scope
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
					state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
				}
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					OR = {
						c:CON ?= owner
						c:AIT ?= owner
						c:TUG ?= owner
						c:MAS ?= owner
					}
					NOT = { owner = { is_subject_of = c:FRA } }
					owner = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:neutral }
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = algerian_raided_state_scope
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						c:CON ?= owner
						c:AIT ?= owner
						c:TUG ?= owner
						c:MAS ?= owner
					}
					NOT = { owner = { is_subject_of = c:FRA } }
					owner = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:neutral }
				}
				owner = { save_scope_as = algerian_raider_scope }
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				is_ruler = no
				is_heir = no
			}
			save_scope_as = algerian_raid_general_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:algerian_raided_state_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = algerian_state_raided
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:algerian_raider_scope
			value = -20
		}
		scope:algerian_raider_scope = {
			post_notification = algerian_raid
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.5.b
		scope:algerian_raided_state_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = algerian_state_raided
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = algeria_events.5.tt
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					scope:algerian_raid_general_scope = {
						kill_character = yes
					}
				}
				50 = {
				}
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:algerian_raider_scope
			value = -20
		}
		scope:algerian_raider_scope = {
			post_notification = algerian_raid
		}
	}
}

algeria_events.6 = {

		NOT = { has_variable = arab_bureaux_var }
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs }

			has_role = general

			is_heir = no

			is_incorporated = yes
			has_building = building_university
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				is_ruler = no

				is_incorporated = yes

		}

					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS

					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN

					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = arab_bureaux_peacekeepers_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = algeria_events.6.b
		scope:arab_bureaux_university_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = arab_bureaux_university_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
					state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = arab_bureaux_administrators_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {

		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS

algeria_events.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:algeria_algiers_state_scope
	title = algeria_events.8.t
	desc = algeria_events.8.d
	flavor = algeria_events.8.f

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_city_center"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	trigger = {
	# triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
			}
			save_scope_as = algeria_algiers_state_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.8.a
		highlighted_option = yes
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = algeria_events.8.tt.1
				has_variable = algeria_constantine_var
				has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
			}
		}
		set_variable = algeria_deylik_restored
		set_capital = STATE_ALGIERS
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.1
			culture = cu:turkish
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = barbary_pirates_modifier
			days = very_long_modifier_time
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.8.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = algeria_events.8.tt.1
				has_variable = algeria_constantine_var
				has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				exists = c:TUR
				c:TUR ?= {
					has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = algeria_deylik_restored
		set_capital = STATE_ALGIERS
		add_primary_culture = cu:turkish
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:TUR ?= {
					is_country_type = recognized
				}
			}
			c:TUR ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = c:ALD
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			c:TUR ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = c:ALD
					type = vassal
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = barbary_pirates_modifier
			days = very_long_modifier_time
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.2
			culture = cu:turkish
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = algeria_events.8.c
		default_option = yes
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = algeria_events.8.tt.2
				has_variable = algeria_mascara_var
			}
		}
		set_variable = algeria_maghreb_restored
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_theocracy
				}
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		s:STATE_AL_RIF = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_FEZ = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_MARRAKECH = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_INNER_MOROCCO = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = algeria_events.8.e
		default_option = yes
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = algeria_events.8.tt.3
				OR = {
					has_variable = algeria_ait_abbas_var
					has_variable = algeria_touggourt_var
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = algeria_barbary_restored
		set_capital = STATE_ALGIERS
		remove_primary_culture = cu:maghrebi
		add_modifier = {
			name = barbary_pirates_modifier
			days = very_long_modifier_time
		}
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_AL_RIF = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_FEZ = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_MARRAKECH = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_INNER_MOROCCO = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = algeria_events.8.g
		ruler = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modern_jugurtha
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.1
			culture = cu:berber
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.1
			culture = cu:maghrebi
		}
		s:STATE_EAST_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
		s:STATE_SAHARA = {
			add_claim = c:ALD
		}
	}

# French Foreign Legion
algeria_events.9 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:algeria_state_scope
	title = algeria_events.9.t
	desc = algeria_events.9.d
	flavor = algeria_events.9.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_construction_colony"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	trigger = {
		# triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_north_africa
				any_scope_building = {
				 	is_building_type = building_barrack
					level >= 20
					occupancy >= 0.5
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = algeria_state_scope
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				save_scope_as = legion_barracks
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = algeria_events.9.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:legion_barracks = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = legion_headquarters
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_divided_monarchists
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = bonapartist_progress_increase_15_tt
				je:je_divided_monarchists = {
					add_progress = { value = 15 name = divided_monarchists_bonapartist_progress_bar }
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			# ig approval and some polstr
			add_modifier = {
				name = french_foreign_legion_ig
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = algeria_events.9.b
		scope:legion_barracks = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = legion_headquarters
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_divided_monarchists
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = bonapartist_progress_increase_5_tt
				je:je_divided_monarchists = {
					add_progress = { value = 5 name = divided_monarchists_bonapartist_progress_bar }
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_north_africa
				any_scope_building = {
				 	is_building_type = building_barrack
					level >= 20
					occupancy >= 0.5
				}
			}
			# migration attraction and training rate
			add_modifier = {
				name = french_foreign_legion_recruitment
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿### Coup Pulse Events

namespace = coup_pulse_events

coup_pulse_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = coup_pulse_events.1.t
	desc = coup_pulse_events.1.d
	flavor = coup_pulse_events.1.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:accused_general

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = general
			is_ruler = no
			NOT = {
				has_variable = golpista_var
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				is_ruler = no
				NOT = {
					has_variable = golpista_var
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = accused_general
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = accused_general_ig
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			scope:accused_general ?= {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
			NOT = { exists = scope:accused_general }
			NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_ip4_coup }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:accused_general_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_persecuted_general
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = coup_pulse_events.1.a.tt
			scope:accused_general = {
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:accused_general_ig = {
					is_same_interest_group_type = scope:golpista_ig
				}
			}
			je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
				add_progress = { value = -15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
			}
			custom_tooltip = coup_retreat_15_tt
		}
		else = {
			je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
				add_progress = { value = 15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
			}
			custom_tooltip = coup_advance_15_tt
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.1.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_uncertain_loyalties_country
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:accused_general = {
					interest_group = {
						is_same_interest_group_type = scope:golpista_ig
					}
				}
			}
			je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
				add_progress = { value = 15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
			}
			custom_tooltip = coup_advance_15_tt
		}
		else = {
			je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
				add_progress = { value = -15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
			}
			custom_tooltip = coup_retreat_15_tt
		}
	}
}

coup_pulse_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = coup_pulse_events.2.t
	desc = coup_pulse_events.2.d
	flavor = coup_pulse_events.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_active_party = {
			AND = {
				has_party_member = scope:golpista_ig
				any_member = {
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_active_party = {
			limit = {
				has_party_member = scope:golpista_ig
				any_member = {
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
			random_member = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = no
				}
				save_scope_as = conflicted_loyalty_ig
			}
			save_scope_as = conflicted_loyalty_party
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_journal_entry = je_ip4_coup
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:conflicted_loyalty_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_appeased_regarding_coup
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
			add_progress = { value = -15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
		}
		custom_tooltip = coup_retreat_15_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.2.b
		scope:conflicted_loyalty_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_conflicted_about_coup
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

coup_pulse_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = coup_pulse_events.3.t
	desc = coup_pulse_events.3.d
	flavor = coup_pulse_events.3.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_interest_group = {
			NOT = { scope:golpista_ig ?= this }
			ig_approval >= -9
			is_in_government = no
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:golpista_ig ?= this }
				ig_approval >= -9
				is_in_government = no
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
			save_scope_as = critical_support_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_journal_entry = je_ip4_coup
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:critical_support_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_critical_support_against_coup
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
			add_progress = { value = -15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
		}
		custom_tooltip = coup_retreat_15_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.3.b
		scope:critical_support_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_critical_support_denied
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		capital = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				strata = upper
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
		}
		custom_tooltip = coup_advance_15_tt
	}
}

coup_pulse_events.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:investor_affected_state

	title = coup_pulse_events.4.t
	desc = coup_pulse_events.4.d
	flavor = coup_pulse_events.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_factory_closed"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_industry.dds"

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		capital = {
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_group = bg_light_industry
					is_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
				}
			}
		}
		scope:golpista_ig = {
			NOT = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = investor_affected_state
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			save_scope_as = investor_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_journal_entry = je_ip4_coup
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_wary_investors_country
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
			add_progress = { value = -10 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
		}
		custom_tooltip = coup_retreat_10_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.4.b
		capital = {
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_group = bg_light_industry
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = modifier_wary_investors_building
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_wary_investors_building
					months = 12
				}
			}
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = modifier_wary_investors_building
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_wary_investors_building
					months = 12
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_economic_uncertainty
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 10 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
		}
		custom_tooltip = coup_advance_10_tt
	}
}

			add_progress = { value = 15 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
		}
		custom_tooltip = coup_advance_15_tt
	}
}

coup_pulse_events.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = coup_pulse_events.8.t
	desc = coup_pulse_events.8.d
	flavor = coup_pulse_events.8.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_conspiring"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = coup_sponsor
		}
		any_country = {
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
			has_interest_marker_in_region = root.capital.region
			relations:root >= 0
			any_interest_group = {
				is_in_government = yes
				is_same_interest_group_type = scope:golpista_ig
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
			NOT = {
				this = root
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_variable = coup_sponsor
			NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_ip4_coup }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				has_interest_marker_in_region = root.capital.region
				relations:root >= 0
				any_interest_group = {
					is_in_government = yes
					is_same_interest_group_type = scope:golpista_ig
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				}
				NOT = {
					this = root
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = coup_sponsor_country
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_journal_entry = je_ip4_coup
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.8.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = coup_sponsor_tt
		je:je_ip4_coup ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 25 name = je_ip4_coup_progress_bar }
		}
		custom_tooltip = coup_advance_25_tt
		ROOT = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_sponsor value = scope:coup_sponsor_country }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = coup_pulse_events.8.b
		scope:golpista_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_rejected_foreign_influence_coup
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

namespace = coup_aftermath_events

coup_aftermath_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:legacy_of_democracy_state

	title = coup_aftermath_events.1.t
	desc = coup_aftermath_events.1.d
	flavor = coup_aftermath_events.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_political_extremism"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_variable = coup_aftermath_var
		country_has_voting_franchise = no
		any_interest_group = {
			law_enactment_stance = {
				law = law_type:law_census_voting # At the least
				value > neutral
			}
			is_in_government = no
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			NOT = {
				is_capital = yes
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				law_enactment_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_census_voting # At the least
					value > neutral
				}
				is_in_government = no
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
			save_scope_as = legacy_of_democracy_ig
		}
		capital = {
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = ROOT
				}
				save_scope_as = legacy_of_democracy_state
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = coup_leader
		}
		remove_variable = coup_aftermath_var
	}

	option = {
		name = coup_aftermath_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:legacy_of_democracy_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_legacy_of_democratic_rule_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:legacy_of_democracy_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_legacy_of_democratic_rule_state
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = coup_aftermath_events.1.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_legacy_of_democratic_rule_country
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:legacy_of_democracy_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		capital = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		capital = {
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = ROOT
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					value = small_radicals
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

﻿namespace = dreyfus

# Dreyfus conviction event
dreyfus.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = dreyfus.1.t
	desc = dreyfus.1.d
	flavor = dreyfus.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_hostile_court"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		c:FRA ?= ROOT
		has_technology_researched = political_agitation
		OR = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = recently_lost_war
				AND = {
					has_variable = recently_had_war
					NOT = {
						has_variable = recently_won_war
					}
				}
			}
			any_state = {
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:french
				}
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_SAVOY
						AND = {
							state_region = s:STATE_PROVENCE
							state_region = {
								any_scope_state = {
									owner = root
								}
							}
							is_split_state = yes
						}
					}
					owner = root
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_multicultural
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = dreyfus_convicted
		}
		any_scope_state = {
        	state_region = {
        		is_homeland = cu:french
        	}
        	any_scope_pop = {
        		religion = rel:jewish
        	}
		}
		any_country = {
			has_diplomatic_pact = {
				who = root
				type = rivalry
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = canonic_dreyfus_character_template
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
			on_created = {
				place_character_in_void = 10000
				set_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = rivalry
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_rival_country_scope
			set_variable = dreyfus_rival_country_var
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_scope
		}
		set_variable = dreyfus_convicted
	}

	option = {
		name = dreyfus.1.a
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_GUAYANA
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus.1.tt_1
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = army_honor_defended
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_autonomous_army
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = anti_semitic_demonstrations
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = dreyfus.2 years = 2 popup = yes }
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				scope:alfred_dreyfus_scope = {
					add_trait = psychological_affliction
				}
			}
			50 = {
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.1.b
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus.1.tt_2
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = army_honor_defended
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_autonomous_army
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = anti_semitic_demonstrations
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = dreyfus.2 years = 2 popup = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.1.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
				is_in_government = no
			}
			institution_investment_level = {
				institution = institution_home_affairs
				value >= 2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus.1.tt_3
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_subject_of_inquiry
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_autonomous_army
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = anti_semitic_demonstrations
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = dreyfus.2 years = 2 popup = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus.1.tt_4
			set_variable = dreyfus_early_investigation
		}
	}
}

# Esterhazy trial
dreyfus.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	desc = dreyfus.2.d

	}

		# Triggered by dreyfus.1
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_scope
		}
		create_character = {
			template = canonic_esterhazy_character_template
			save_scope_as = ferdinand_esterhazy_scope
			on_created = {
				place_character_in_void = 10000
				set_variable = ferdinand_esterhazy_var
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = dreyfus_rival_country_var
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_rival_country_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = dreyfus.2.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger_event = { id = dreyfus.3 days = 7 popup = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
					has_modifier = army_honor_defended
				}
			}
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
				remove_modifier = army_honor_defended
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.2.b
		trigger_event = { id = dreyfus.3 days = 7 popup = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
					has_modifier = dreyfus_autonomous_army
				}
			}
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
				remove_modifier = dreyfus_autonomous_army
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_wavering_army
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# J'Accuse...!
dreyfus.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = dreyfus.3.t
	desc = dreyfus.3.d
	flavor = dreyfus.3.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_conspiring"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by dreyfus.2
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_leader_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = yes
					is_in_government = yes
					ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					is_in_government = no
					ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
				}
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = emile_zola_scope
					set_variable = emile_zola_var
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = canonic_zola_character_template
				save_scope_as = emile_zola_scope
				on_created = {
					set_variable = emile_zola_var
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = dreyfus.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_dreyfus_affair
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.3.b
		default_option = yes
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = dreyfus.3.tt_2
				has_variable = dreyfus_early_investigation
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_dreyfus_affair
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus.3.tt_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

# At-home Asphyxiation
dreyfus.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = dreyfus.4.t
	desc = dreyfus.4.d
	flavor = dreyfus.4.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_agitators_sneaking"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_variable = emile_zola_var
				is_busy = no
			}
			any_interest_group = {
				ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
				leader = {
					is_busy = no
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_variable = zola_asphyxiation }
		var:rennes_retrial_var >= 35
	}

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_scope
		}
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_variable = emile_zola_var
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = emile_zola_var
					is_busy = no
				}
				save_scope_as = emile_zola_scope
				interest_group = {
					save_scope_as = zola_asphyxiation_ig_pro
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
					leader = {
						is_busy = no
					}
				}
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = zola_asphyxiation_leader
				}
				save_scope_as = zola_asphyxiation_ig_pro
			}
		}
		set_variable = zola_asphyxiation
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
					NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces }
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
					NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces }
				}
				save_scope_as = zola_asphyxiation_ig_anti
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = dreyfus.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:zola_asphyxiation_leader = {
			kill_character = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_decrease_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = -1
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.4.b
		scope:zola_asphyxiation_leader = {
			kill_character = yes
		}
		scope:zola_asphyxiation_ig_pro = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = zola_state_funeral_positive
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:dreyfus_armed_forces_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = zola_state_funeral_negative
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:zola_asphyxiation_ig_anti
			}
			scope:zola_asphyxiation_ig_anti = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = zola_state_funeral_negative
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = zola_state_funeral_expenses
			days = short_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_medium
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.4.c
		scope:zola_asphyxiation_leader = {
			kill_character = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

# Émile Zola accused of libel
dreyfus.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = dreyfus.5.t
	desc = dreyfus.5.d
	flavor = dreyfus.5.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_hostile_court"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			has_variable = emile_zola_var
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_leader_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = emile_zola_var
			}
			save_scope_as = emile_zola_scope
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = emile_zola_ig_scope
			}
		}
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = emile_zola_libel_state_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = dreyfus.5.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_decrease_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = -1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = dreyfus.5.tt_1
		random_list = {
			90 = {
				scope:emile_zola_scope = {
					exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
				}
			}
			10 = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = dreyfus.5.tt_2
					scope:emile_zola_scope = {
						kill_character = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:emile_zola_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_outraged_by_court_desicion
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_pleased_with_court_desicion
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.5.b
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_imperfect_compromise
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:emile_zola_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_imperfect_compromise
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.5.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_outraged_by_court_desicion
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:emile_zola_libel_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state  = {
				pop_type = officers
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

# Dreyfusard Board Game Released
dreyfus.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:dreyfus_parlor_game_state
	title = dreyfus.6.t
	desc = dreyfus.6.d
	flavor = dreyfus.6.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_world_fair"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_variable = dreyfusard_board_game_var }
		any_interest_group = {
			OR = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
				is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
			}
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			is_in_government = no
			ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
		}
		var:rennes_retrial_var >= 15
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_parlor_game_state
		}
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
		}
		set_variable = dreyfusard_board_game_var
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_leader_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_armed_forces_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
					is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
					is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
				}
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				is_in_government = no
				ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_parlor_game_ig_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = dreyfus.6.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_not_fun_and_games
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:dreyfus_parlor_game_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state  = {
				pop_type = officers
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.6.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_protected_speech
			}
		}
		scope:dreyfus_parlor_game_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = parlor_game_aggresive_censorship
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:dreyfus_parlor_game_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state  = {
				strata = upper
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

# Anti Dreyfusard Riot
dreyfus.7 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_state
	title = dreyfus.7.t
	desc = dreyfus.7.d
	flavor = dreyfus.7.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_political_extremism"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	trigger = {
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_pop = {
				religion = rel:jewish
			}
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
			culture = {
				save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_culture
			}
			religion = {
				save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_religion
			}
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = anti_dreyfusard_riot_leader
			}
			save_scope_as = anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig = {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
					}
				}
				ig:ig_devout ?= {
					ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
				}
			}
			ig:ig_devout ?= {
				save_scope_as = anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig_devout
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_pop = {
					religion = rel:jewish
				}
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					religion = rel:jewish
				}
				save_scope_as = anti_dreyfusard_riot_pop
			}
			save_scope_as = anti_dreyfusard_riot_state
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = dreyfus.7.a
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_decrease_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = -1
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = dreyfus_slipping_public_order
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig_devout
			}
			scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig_devout = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = dreyfus_vox_dei
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_vox_populi
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:jewish
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.7.b
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = dreyfus_slipping_public_order
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig_devout
			}
			scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig_devout = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = dreyfus_street_brawls
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_street_brawls
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:catholic
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.7.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			institution_investment_level = {
				institution = institution_police
				value >= 1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = dreyfus_slipping_public_order
			days = short_modifier_time
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig_devout
			}
			scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig_devout = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = dreyfus_street_brawls
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = dreyfus_street_brawls
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = rel:catholic
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		scope:anti_dreyfusard_riot_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				religion = rel:jewish
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

# The Retrial
dreyfus.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = dreyfus.8.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = dreyfus.8.d_1
				trigger = {
					has_variable = dreyfus_trial_exoneration
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = dreyfus.8.d_2
				trigger = {
					has_variable = dreyfus_trial_lesser_sentence
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = dreyfus.8.d_3
				trigger = {
					has_variable = dreyfus_trial_reincarceration
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = dreyfus.8.f_1
				trigger = {
					has_variable = dreyfus_trial_exoneration
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = dreyfus.8.f_2
				trigger = {
					has_variable = dreyfus_trial_lesser_sentence

# Esterhazy admits guilt in interview
dreyfus.9 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = dreyfus.9.t
	desc = dreyfus.9.d
	flavor = dreyfus.9.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_middleclass_cafe"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	trigger = {
		any_country = {
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
			NOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized }
		}
		var:rennes_retrial_var >= 30
	}
	immediate = {
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
			}
			save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
		}
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = ferdinand_esterhazy_var
			}
			save_scope_as = ferdinand_esterhazy_scope
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				NOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized }
			}
			save_scope_as = ferdinand_esterhazy_country_scope
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = dreyfus_rival_country_var
			}
			save_scope_as = dreyfus_rival_country_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.9.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_2
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = dreyfus_aknowledged_evidence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.9.b
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dreyfus_case_strength_increase_by_1
			change_variable = {
				name = dreyfus_case_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:ferdinand_esterhazy_country_scope
			value = -20
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = dreyfus_truth_relative
					days = normal_modifier_time
					multiplier = 0.5
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = dreyfus.9.c
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_dreyfusard = yes
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = dreyfus_truth_relative
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					ig_is_anti_dreyfusard = yes
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = dreyfus_truth_relative_positive
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

exiles_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_events.1.t
	desc = exiles_events.1.d
	flavor = exiles_events.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_gunboat_diplomacy"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = agitator
			character_supports_political_movement = no
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:exile_scope
			}
			scope:exile_scope ?= {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = agitator
				character_supports_political_movement = no
			}
			this.interest_group ?= {
				save_scope_as = agitator_ig
			}
			save_scope_as = agitator_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:agitator_scope = {
			exile_character = yes
		}
		scope:agitator_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_agitator_left
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.1.b
		scope:agitator_scope = {
			exile_character = yes
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_agitator_hopeless
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
			random_list = {
				30 = {
					add_trait = alcoholic
				}
				70 = {

				}
			}
		}
	}
}

exiles_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_events.2.t
	desc = exiles_events.2.d
	flavor = exiles_events.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_middleclass_cafe"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		any_scope_character = {
			is_ruler = no
			is_heir = no
			this.interest_group ?= {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			this.interest_group_type = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig_type
			}
		}
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			character_acceptance = { target = prev value >= acceptance_status_4 }
			this.interest_group_type = {
				this = scope:ex_ig_type
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:exile_scope
			}
			scope:exile_scope ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = no
				is_heir = no
				this.interest_group ?= {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					this.interest_group_type = prev.interest_group_type
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = friend_scope
			this.interest_group ?= {
				save_scope_as = exile_ig_scope
			}
			this.interest_group_type = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig_type
			}
		}
		random_character_in_exile_pool = {
			limit = {
				character_acceptance = { target = prev value >= acceptance_status_4 }
				this.interest_group_type = {
					this = scope:ex_ig_type
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = exile_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:exile_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_exile_ig_empowered
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = root
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.2.b
		scope:friend_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_judgement_questioned
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			this.interest_group ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_judgement_questioned_ig
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

exiles_events.3 = {
	type = country_event

	duration = 3

	desc = exiles_events.3.d

	}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		any_political_movement = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = movement_scope
		}
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			root = this.home_country
			could_support_political_movement = scope:movement_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:exile_scope
			}
			scope:exile_scope ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					root = this.home_country
					could_support_political_movement = prev
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = movement_scope
		}
		random_character_in_exile_pool = {
			limit = {
				root = this.home_country
				could_support_political_movement = scope:movement_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = exile_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = root
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}
		scope:movement_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_agitator_invited
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.3.b
		scope:movement_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_agitator_refused
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:exile_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_agitator_coward
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

	cooldown = { days = long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_interest_group = {
			OR = {
				has_modifier = failed_coup
				has_modifier = revolution_lost_ig

			}
			any_scope_character = {
				OR = {
					has_role = agitator
					has_modifier = incompetent_coup_attempt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:failed_revolution_agitator
			}
			scope:failed_revolution_agitator ?= {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_modifier = failed_coup
					has_modifier = revolution_lost_ig
					has_variable = coup_guilty
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = failed_revolution_ig
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_role = agitator
						has_modifier = incompetent_coup_attempt
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = failed_revolution_agitator
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:failed_revolution_agitator = {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.4.b
		scope:failed_revolution_agitator = {
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
				}
				33 = {
					add_trait = wounded
					exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
				}
				33 = {
					kill_character = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

exiles_events.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_events.5.t
	desc = exiles_events.5.d
	flavor = exiles_events.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_signed_contract"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_approval >= 5
			type = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig_type
			}
		}
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			root = this.home_country
			interest_group_type = scope:ex_ig_type
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:exile_scope
			}
			scope:exile_scope ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_approval >= 5
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					root = this.home_country
					this.interest_group_type = prev.type
				}
			}
			type = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig_type
			}
			save_scope_as = exile_ig
		}
		random_character_in_exile_pool = {
			limit = {
				root = this.home_country
				this.interest_group_type = scope:ex_ig_type
			}
			save_scope_as = exile_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = root
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_accepted_repentant_exile
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_events.5.b
		scope:exile_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_refused_repentant_exile
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
		has_technology_researched = egalitarianism #adding this because otherwise this event fires predictably early game near every playthrough. Fits with text.
	}

			limit = {

					could_support_political_movement = prev
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = movement_scope
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					could_support_political_movement = prev
				}

				}
			}
		}
	}
