﻿namespace = exiles_more_events

exiles_more_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.1.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.1.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_armored_train"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_diplomatic_play = {
			is_war = yes
			target_is = ROOT
		}

		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			home_country ?= ROOT
			save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_type_matches_former = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_exile_pool  = {
			limit = {
				home_country ?= ROOT
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
			save_scope_as = patriotic_exile_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_type_matches_former = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = patriotic_exile_home_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:patriotic_exile_scope
			}
			scope:patriotic_exile_scope ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.1.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:patriotic_exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = ROOT
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}

		scope:patriotic_exile_home_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = exile_pardoned
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.1.b

		scope:patriotic_exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = ROOT
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}

		scope:patriotic_exile_scope = {
			add_character_role = general
		}

		scope:patriotic_exile_home_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = exile_welcomed_and_promoted
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.1.c

		# absolutely refuse to let exile back

		scope:patriotic_exile_home_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = exile_offer_rejected
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Exile attempts to return to break law passage deadlock
exiles_more_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.2.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.2.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.2.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_gunboat_diplomacy"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		enacting_any_law = yes

		OR = {
			AND = {
				any_interest_group = {
					law_stance = {
						law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
						value = strongly_approve
					}
					leader = {
						NOT = {
							has_modifier = ideological_victor
						}
					}
					save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
				}
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					home_country ?= ROOT
					ig_type_matches_former = yes
					law_stance = {
						law = root.currently_enacting_law.type
						value = strongly_approve
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				any_interest_group = {
					law_stance = {
						law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
						value = strongly_disapprove
					}
					leader = {
						NOT = {
							has_modifier = ideological_victor
						}
					}
					save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
				}
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					home_country ?= ROOT
					ig_type_matches_former = yes
					law_stance = {
						law = root.currently_enacting_law.type
						value = strongly_disapprove
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = interventionist_exile_law_scope
		}

		random_character_in_exile_pool = {
			limit = {
				home_country ?= root
				OR = {
					AND = {
						law_stance = {
							law = root.currently_enacting_law.type
							value = strongly_approve
						}
						interest_group_type = {
							save_temporary_scope_as = exile_ig_type
						}
						root = {
							any_interest_group = {
								this.type = scope:exile_ig_type
								law_stance = {
									law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
									value = strongly_approve
								}
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						law_stance = {
							law = root.currently_enacting_law.type
							value = strongly_disapprove
						}
						interest_group_type = {
							save_temporary_scope_as = exile_ig_type
						}
						root = {
							any_interest_group = {
								this.type = scope:exile_ig_type
								law_stance = {
									law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
									value = strongly_disapprove
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
			save_scope_as = interventionist_exile_scope
		}

		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_type_matches_former = yes
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = interventionist_exile_ig_leader
			}
			save_scope_as = interventionist_exile_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:interventionist_exile_scope
			}
			scope:interventionist_exile_scope ?= {
				OR = {
					is_in_exile_pool = no
					is_character_alive = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.2.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:interventionist_exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = ROOT
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.2.b

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:interventionist_exile_ig = {
					law_stance = {
						law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
						value = strongly_approve
					}
				}
			}
			add_enactment_modifier = {
				name = positive_exile_intervention
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:interventionist_exile_ig = {
					law_stance = {
						law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
						value = strongly_disapprove
					}
				}
			}
			add_enactment_modifier = {
				name = negative_exile_intervention
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.2.c
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = exiles_more_events.2.c.tt
				OR = {
					AND = {
						scope:interventionist_exile_scope = { has_liberal_ideology = yes }
						scope:interventionist_exile_ig = { leader = { has_liberal_ideology = no } }
					}
					AND = {
						scope:interventionist_exile_scope = { has_progressive_ideology = yes }
						scope:interventionist_exile_ig = { leader = { has_progressive_ideology = no } }
					}
					AND = {
						scope:interventionist_exile_scope = { has_socialist_ideology = yes }
						scope:interventionist_exile_ig = { leader = { has_socialist_ideology = no } }
					}
					AND = {
						scope:interventionist_exile_scope = { has_reactionary_ideology = yes }
						scope:interventionist_exile_ig = { leader = { has_reactionary_ideology = no } }
					}
					AND = {
						scope:interventionist_exile_scope = { has_conservative_ideology = yes }
						scope:interventionist_exile_ig = { leader = { has_conservative_ideology = no } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		scope:interventionist_exile_ig = {
			leader = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = ideological_victor
					days = long_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Agitator has no domestic support and sullenly goes into self-exile
exiles_more_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.3.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.3.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.3.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = agitator
			character_supports_political_movement = yes
			political_movement ?= {
				political_movement_support < 0.1
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = agitator
				character_supports_political_movement = yes
				political_movement ?= {
					political_movement_support < 0.1
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = the_loneliest_little_agitator
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = the_loneliest_little_agitator_ig
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator
			}
			scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator ?= {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.3.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
			exile_character = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Perhaps it's time for a little study and reflection?
		name = exiles_more_events.3.b
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
				has_trait = erudite
			}
		}

		scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = inspired_by_studies
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # time for something radical
		name = exiles_more_events.3.c
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
				not = { has_trait = cautious }
				character_supports_political_movement = yes
			}
		}

		random_list = {
			1 = {
				scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
					political_movement ?= {
						add_modifier = {
							name = radical_new_approach
							days = normal_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
					political_movement ?= {
						add_modifier = {
							name = failed_relaunch
							days = normal_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
					political_movement ?= {
						add_modifier = {
							name = all_worked_up
							days = normal_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # There's no need for anything drastic like exile!
		name = exiles_more_events.3.dd
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
				has_trait = cautious
				character_supports_political_movement = yes
			}
		}

		scope:the_loneliest_little_agitator = {
			political_movement ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = deliberate_deliberations
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Nationalist exile rallies to the flag of a unifier
exiles_more_events.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.4.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.4.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_steam_ship"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_major_unifier = yes
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			NOT = { home_country ?= ROOT }
			culture = {
				is_primary_culture_of = ROOT
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_exile_pool  = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { home_country ?= ROOT }
				culture = {
					is_primary_culture_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = post_unification_agitator
			home_country ?= {
				save_scope_as = post_unification_agitator_home
			}
			ideology = {
				save_scope_as = post_unification_agitator_ideology
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:post_unification_agitator
			}
			scope:post_unification_agitator ?= {
				OR = {
					is_in_exile_pool = no
					is_character_alive = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.4.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:post_unification_agitator = {
			transfer_character = ROOT
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_liberal_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_liberal_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rises
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_progressive_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_progressive_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rises
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_socialist_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_socialist_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rises
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_reactionary_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_reactionary_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rises
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_conservative_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_conservative_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rises
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.4.b

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_liberal_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_liberal_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_progressive_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_progressive_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_socialist_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_socialist_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_reactionary_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_reactionary_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { has_conservative_ideology = yes }
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = { has_conservative_ideology = yes }
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		# ULTIMATE MANUAL FALLBACKS

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces }
			}
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout }
			}
			ig:ig_devout ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists }
			}
			ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia }
			}
			ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners }
			}
			ig:ig_landowners ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie }
			}
			ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk }
			}
			ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:post_unification_agitator = { is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions }
			}
			ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = national_hero_rejected
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Previously-exiled character goes back into exile
exiles_more_events.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.5.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.5.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_gunboat_diplomacy"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			was_exiled = yes
			popularity < 0
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				was_exiled = yes
				popularity < 0
			}
			save_scope_as = experienced_exile
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:experienced_exile
			}
			scope:experienced_exile ?= {
				OR = {
					is_in_exile_pool = no
					is_character_alive = no
				}
			}
		}
	}


	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.5.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:experienced_exile = {
			exile_character = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.5.b

		random_list = {
			1 = {
				scope:experienced_exile = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = filled_with_determination
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				scope:experienced_exile = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = dejected_tryhard
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# THE CALAMITOUS RETURN OF THE VILE [character name]
exiles_more_events.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.6.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.6.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.6.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:melodrama_villain

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			home_country ?= ROOT
			OR = {
				has_trait = arrogant
				has_trait = reckless
				has_trait = ambitious
				has_trait = imperious
				has_trait = wrathful
				has_trait = cruel
				has_trait = imposing
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_type_matches_former = yes
			ig_approval < 0
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_exile_pool  = {
			limit = {
				home_country ?= ROOT
				OR = {
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = reckless
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = imperious
					has_trait = wrathful
					has_trait = cruel
					has_trait = imposing
				}
			}
			ideology = {
				save_scope_as = melodrama_villain_ideology
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
			save_scope_as = melodrama_villain
			set_variable = {
				name = THE_NEFARIOUS_TOP_HAT
			}
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_type_matches_former = yes
				ig_approval < 0
			}
			save_scope_as = melodrama_villain_ig
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = melodrama_villain_ig_leader
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:melodrama_villain
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:melodrama_villain_ig_leader
			}
			scope:melodrama_villain ?= {
				OR = {
					is_in_exile_pool = no
					is_character_alive = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.6.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:melodrama_villain ?= {
			transfer_character = ROOT
			remove_variable = THE_NEFARIOUS_TOP_HAT
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}

		scope:melodrama_villain_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = in_the_thrall_of_a_villain
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.6.b
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			scope:melodrama_villain_ig_leader ?= {
				OR = {
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = reckless
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = imperious
					has_trait = wrathful
					has_trait = cruel
					has_trait = imposing
				}
			}
		}

		scope:melodrama_villain_ig_leader ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = apprentice_becomes_the_master
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:melodrama_villain ?= {
			remove_variable = THE_NEFARIOUS_TOP_HAT
			add_modifier = {
				name = foiled_again
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}


	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.6.c
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			scope:melodrama_villain = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = reckless
				}
			}
		}

		scope:melodrama_villain ?= {
			remove_variable = THE_NEFARIOUS_TOP_HAT
		}

		random_list = {
			1 = {
				scope:melodrama_villain = {
					transfer_character = ROOT
					hidden_effect = {
						free_from_exile_effect = yes
					}
				}

				scope:melodrama_villain = {
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes

					custom_tooltip = {
						text = exiles_more_events.6.c.tt
						set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					}

					add_modifier = {
						name = i_return_triumphant
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				scope:melodrama_villain = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = foiled_again
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.6.dd

		if = {
			limit = { scope:melodrama_villain_ig_leader ?= { NOT = { has_ideology = scope:melodrama_villain_ideology } } }
			scope:melodrama_villain_ig_leader ?= { set_ideology = scope:melodrama_villain_ideology }
		}
		else = {
			scope:melodrama_villain_ig = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = pawns_of_a_greater_mind
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		scope:melodrama_villain ?= {
			remove_variable = THE_NEFARIOUS_TOP_HAT
		}
	}
}

# Two competing exiles living in ROOT drive one or the other out of the country because they brought their problems with them
exiles_more_events.7 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.7.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.7.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.7.f

	gui_window = event_window_2char

	left_icon = scope:first_ex_exile
	right_icon = scope:second_ex_exile

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/2Characters"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			NOT = { home_country ?= ROOT }
			was_exiled = yes
			count >= 2
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { home_country ?= ROOT }
				was_exiled = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = first_ex_exile
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { home_country ?= ROOT }
				was_exiled = yes
				NOT = { scope:first_ex_exile = this }
			}
			save_scope_as = second_ex_exile
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:first_ex_exile
			}
			scope:first_ex_exile ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:second_ex_exile
			}
			scope:second_ex_exile ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.7.a
		default_option = yes

		add_modifier = {
			name = their_spats_are_our_problems
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.7.b
		trigger = {
			scope:first_ex_exile = {
				is_ruler = no
			}
		}
		scope:first_ex_exile = {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.7.c
		trigger = {
			scope:second_ex_exile = {
				is_ruler = no
			}
		}
		scope:second_ex_exile = {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
	}
}

# Exile returns because legitimacy is so low that no one cares
exiles_more_events.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.8.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.8.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.8.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_trains"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		government_legitimacy < 25
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			home_country ?= ROOT
			save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_type_matches_former = yes
			is_in_government = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_exile_pool  = {
			limit = {
				home_country ?= ROOT
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ex_ig
			save_scope_as = dgaf_exile_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_type_matches_former = yes
				is_in_government = no
			}
			save_scope_as = dgaf_exile_home_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:dgaf_exile_scope
			}
			scope:dgaf_exile_scope ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.8.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:dgaf_exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = ROOT
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}


	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.8.b
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties }
			institution_investment_level = {
				institution = institution_home_affairs
				value >= 2
			}
		}

		custom_tooltip = exiles_more_events.8.b.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.8.c

		add_modifier = {
			name = bargain_with_the_opposition
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		scope:dgaf_exile_home_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = bargain_with_the_government
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Foreign exile decides to set up shop in ROOT
exiles_more_events.9 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.9.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.9.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.9.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_steam_ship"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
			NOT = { home_country ?= ROOT }
			character_acceptance = { target = ROOT value < acceptance_status_5 }
			interest_group_type = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = exile_ig_type
			}
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			scope:exile_ig_type = this.type
			is_powerful = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_character_in_exile_pool  = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { home_country ?= ROOT }
				character_acceptance = { target = ROOT value < acceptance_status_5 }
			}
			interest_group_type = {
				save_scope_as = exile_ig_type
			}
			save_scope_as = fertile_ground_exile_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				scope:exile_ig_type ?= this.type
				is_powerful = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = fertile_ground_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:fertile_ground_exile_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:fertile_ground_ig
			}
			scope:fertile_ground_exile_scope ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.9.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:fertile_ground_exile_scope = {
			transfer_character = ROOT
			hidden_effect = {
				free_from_exile_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.9.b

		scope:fertile_ground_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = messed_with_the_wrong_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.9.c
		highlighted_option = yes

		trigger = {
			scope:fertile_ground_ig = {
				is_in_government = no
			}
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_censorship
			}
		}

		scope:fertile_ground_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = accused_of_subversion
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Purging unstable elements in a time of crisis
exiles_more_events.10 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = exiles_more_events.10.t
	desc = exiles_more_events.10.d
	flavor = exiles_more_events.10.f

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_police_breaking_door"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_revolution_ongoing = yes
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = agitator
			interest_group = { # Loyal, but in opposition
				is_insurrectionary = no
				is_in_government = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = agitator
				interest_group = {
					is_insurrectionary = no
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = unstable_element_agitator
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = unstable_element_ig
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:unstable_element_agitator
			}
			scope:unstable_element_agitator ?= {
				OR = {
					is_character_alive = no
					is_in_exile_pool = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.10.a
		default_option = yes

		scope:unstable_element_agitator = {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
		scope:unstable_element_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = target_of_paranoia
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = exiles_more_events.10.b

		scope:unstable_element_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = the_velvet_glove_forgives
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = garibaldi_events

# Garibaldi Arrives
garibaldi_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_garibaldi"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	title = garibaldi_events.1.t
	desc = garibaldi_events.1.d
	flavor = garibaldi_events.1.f

	cooldown = { days = short_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		year <= 1862 # Garibaldi was (sadly) not immortal
		NOT = {
			has_variable = rejected_garibaldi
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_variable = garibaldi_var
			}
		}
		OR = { # Either Garibaldi is "dead" or he is an exile
			NOT = {
				has_global_variable = garibaldi_is_alive_global_var
			}
			any_character_in_exile_pool = {
				has_variable = garibaldi_var
			}
		}
		OR = {
			AND = { # You're a nation fighting the good cause (defensive war OR democratically-inclined revolution/secession)
				OR = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_anarchy
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_wealth_voting
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_landed_voting
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						OR = {
							is_revolutionary = yes
							is_secessionist = yes
						}
						any_diplomatic_play = {
							is_war = yes
							initiator = root
							target = {
								OR = {
									has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
									has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_oligarchy
									has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_single_party_state
								}
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						any_diplomatic_play = {
							is_war = yes
							target = root
							initiator = {
								is_secessionist = no
								# Garibaldi should only care to get involved defensively against significant revolutions
								OR = {
									is_revolutionary = no
									country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
								}
								# Garibaldi shouldn't be in the business of defending big powers against small ones
								OR = {
									is_revolutionary = yes
									country_rank >= root.country_rank
								}
								OR = {
									has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
									has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_oligarchy
									has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_single_party_state
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { # You're fighting for Italy
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					OR = {
						AND = {
							initiator = {
								any_primary_culture = {
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
								}
								this = root
							}
							target = {
								NOT = {
									any_primary_culture = {
										has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
									}
								}
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = {
										OR = {
											is_homeland = cu:north_italian
											is_homeland = cu:south_italian
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						AND = {
							initiator = {
								any_primary_culture = {
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
								}
								this = root
								is_secessionist = yes
							}
						}
						AND = {
							target = {
								any_primary_culture = {
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
								}
								this = root
							}
							initiator = {
								NOT = {
									any_primary_culture = {
										has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { # You're fighting the Ragamuffin War against Brazil
				c:PNI ?= this
				year <= 1839
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					any_scope_play_involved = {
						c:BRZ ?= this
						has_war_with = root
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_variable = garibaldi_var
					}
					any_character = {
						has_variable = garibaldi_var
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = garibaldi_character_template
				on_created = {
					set_variable = garibaldi_var
					set_variable = {
						name = garibaldi_cooldown_var
						days = short_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = garibaldi_character_template
				}
				save_scope_as = garibaldi_scope
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = garibaldi_var
				}
				transfer_character = root
				save_scope_as = garibaldi_scope
				set_variable = {
					name = garibaldi_cooldown_var
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = global_var:garibaldi_fame_var }
			change_global_variable = {
				name = garibaldi_fame_var
				add = 1
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_global_variable = {
				name = garibaldi_fame_var
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					target = root
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					target = root
				}
				initiator = {
					save_scope_as = garibaldi_spawn_foe_scope
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = root
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = root
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = garibaldi_spawn_foe_scope
				}
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = garibaldi_new_home
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:garibaldi_scope
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = garibaldi_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
			add_character_role = general
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:garibaldi_fame_var = 1
				scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_1
					}
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				add_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:garibaldi_fame_var = 2
				scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_2
					}
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_1
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						remove_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_1
					}
				}
				add_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:garibaldi_fame_var = 3
				scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_3
					}
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_2
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						remove_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_2
					}
				}
				add_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:garibaldi_fame_var = 4
				scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_4
					}
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_3
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						remove_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_3
					}
				}
				add_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_4
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:garibaldi_fame_var >= 5
				scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_5
					}
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_4
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						remove_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_4
					}
				}
				add_modifier = garibaldi_popularity_modifier_tier_5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:garibaldi_fame_var >= 2
				scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
					has_trait = basic_offensive_planner
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						hidden_effect = {
							remove_trait = basic_offensive_planner
						}
						add_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
					}
					50 = {
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:garibaldi_fame_var >= 4
				scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
					has_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						hidden_effect = {
							remove_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
						}
						add_trait = expert_offensive_planner
					}
					50 = {
					}
				}
			}
		}

		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					ROOT = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					has_diplomatic_relevance = ROOT
					has_variable = garibaldi_has_been_here
				}
			}
			post_notification = garibaldi_is_here
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_variable = garibaldi_has_been_here }
				}
				set_variable = garibaldi_has_been_here
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = garibaldi_events.1.b
		set_variable = rejected_garibaldi
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					OR = {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
						is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
						is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
						is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
						is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
					}
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
						is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
						is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
						is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
						is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
					}
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader
						}
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = rejected_garibaldi_modifier
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_variable = garibaldi_var
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope ?= {
				exile_character = yes
				clean_up_exile_character_roles = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
# Garibaldi Fucks Off
garibaldi_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_garibaldi"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = garibaldi_events.2.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = garibaldi_events.2.d_1
				trigger = {
					any_diplomatic_play = {
						is_war = yes
						OR = {
							AND = {
								initiator = {
									any_primary_culture = {
										has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
									}
									NOT = {
										has_variable = rejected_garibaldi
									}
								}
								target = {
									NOT = {
										any_primary_culture = {
											has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
										}
									}
									any_scope_state = {
										state_region = {
											OR = {
												is_homeland = cu:north_italian
												is_homeland = cu:south_italian
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
							AND = {
								target = {
									any_primary_culture = {
										has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
									}
									NOT = {
										has_variable = rejected_garibaldi
									}
								}
								initiator = {
									NOT = {
										any_primary_culture = {
											has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = garibaldi_events.2.d_2
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						any_diplomatic_play = {
							is_war = yes
							OR = {
								AND = {
									initiator = {
										any_primary_culture = {
											has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
										}
										NOT = {
											has_variable = rejected_garibaldi
										}
									}
									target = {
										NOT = {
											any_primary_culture = {
												has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
											}
										}
										any_scope_state = {
											state_region = {
												OR = {
													is_homeland = cu:north_italian
													is_homeland = cu:south_italian
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
								AND = {
									target = {
										any_primary_culture = {
											has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
										}
										NOT = {
											has_variable = rejected_garibaldi
										}
									}
									initiator = {
										NOT = {
											any_primary_culture = {
												has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = garibaldi_events.2.f

	cooldown = { days = short_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		any_scope_character = {
			has_variable = garibaldi_var
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_at_war = no
				NOR = {
					c:ITA ?= this
					any_primary_culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
					}
				}
				any_scope_character = {
					has_variable = garibaldi_var
					NOT = {
						has_variable = garibaldi_cooldown_var
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					OR = {
						AND = {
							initiator = {
								NOT = { this = root }
								any_primary_culture = {
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
								}
								NOT = {
									has_variable = rejected_garibaldi
								}
							}
							target = {
								NOT = {
									any_primary_culture = {
										has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
									}
								}
								any_scope_state = {
									state_region = {
										OR = {
											is_homeland = cu:north_italian
											is_homeland = cu:south_italian
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						AND = {
							target = {
								NOT = { this = root }
								any_primary_culture = {
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
								}
								NOT = {
									has_variable = rejected_garibaldi
								}
							}
							initiator = {
								NOT = {
									any_primary_culture = {
										has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:garibaldi_scope
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = garibaldi_var
			}
			save_scope_as = garibaldi_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					target = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					initiator = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					target = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					initiator = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = garibaldi_italian_nation_scope
				}
				initiator = {
					save_scope_as = garibaldi_foreign_nation_scope
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					target = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = {
								OR = {
									is_homeland = cu:north_italian
									is_homeland = cu:south_italian
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					target = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = {
								OR = {
									is_homeland = cu:north_italian
									is_homeland = cu:south_italian
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				initiator = {
					save_scope_as = garibaldi_italian_nation_scope
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = garibaldi_foreign_nation_scope
				}
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = garibaldi_old_home
	}

	option = {
		name = garibaldi_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:garibaldi_scope = {
					has_modifier = italian_legionaries_modifier
				}
			}
			scope:garibaldi_scope = {
				remove_modifier = italian_legionaries_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_building = {
					has_modifier = italian_legion_barracks_modifier
				}
			}
			every_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = italian_legion_barracks_modifier
				}
				remove_modifier = italian_legion_barracks_modifier
			}
		}
		scope:garibaldi_scope = {
			exile_character = yes
			clean_up_exile_character_roles = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					target = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					initiator = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					target = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					initiator = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
					}
				}
				target = {
					trigger_event = garibaldi_events.1
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					target = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = {
								OR = {
									is_homeland = cu:north_italian
									is_homeland = cu:south_italian
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
						}
					}
					target = {
						NOT = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
							}
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = {
								OR = {
									is_homeland = cu:north_italian
									is_homeland = cu:south_italian
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				initiator = {
					trigger_event = garibaldi_events.1
				}
			}
		}

		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					ROOT = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					has_diplomatic_relevance = ROOT
					has_variable = garibaldi_has_been_here
					scope:garibaldi_italian_nation_scope ?= this
					scope:garibaldi_foreign_nation_scope ?= this
				}
			}
			post_notification = garibaldi_skedaddles
		}
	}
}
# Italian Legion of Volunteers
garibaldi_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:italian_legion_state_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_garibaldi"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	title = garibaldi_events.3.t
	desc = garibaldi_events.3.d
	flavor = garibaldi_events.3.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = italian_legion_var
		}
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		any_scope_character = {
			has_variable = garibaldi_var
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			any_scope_pop = {
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
				}
			}
		}
		any_diplomatic_play = {
			is_war = yes
			OR = {
				initiator = root
				target = root
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = italian_legion_var
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_variable = garibaldi_var
			}
			save_scope_as = garibaldi_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = italian_legion_state_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:italian_legion_state_scope = {
					any_scope_building = {
						is_building_type = building_barrack
					}
				}
			}
			scope:italian_legion_state_scope = {
				random_scope_building = {
					limit = {
						is_building_type = building_barrack
					}
					save_scope_as = italian_legion_barracks_scope
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					target = root
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					target = root
				}
				initiator = {
					save_scope_as = italian_legion_foe_scope
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = root
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					initiator = root
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = italian_legion_foe_scope
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_variable = garibaldi_var
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = garibaldi_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:garibaldi_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = italian_legionaries_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = garibaldi_events.3.b
		trigger = {
			scope:italian_legion_state_scope = {
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
		}
		scope:italian_legion_barracks_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = italian_legion_barracks_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}
# Garibaldi Riles Up Peasants
garibaldi_events.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_garibaldi"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	title = garibaldi_events.4.t
	desc = garibaldi_events.4.d
	flavor = garibaldi_events.4.f

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		any_scope_character = {
			has_variable = garibaldi_var
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_pop = {
				is_pop_type = farmers
			}
		}
		any_diplomatic_play = {
			is_war = yes
			any_scope_play_involved = {
				has_war_with = root
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			is_marginal	= no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = garibaldi_var
			}
			save_scope_as = garibaldi_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
					any_scope_pop = {
					is_pop_type = farmers
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = garibaldi_peasants_state_scope
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				any_scope_play_involved = {
					has_war_with = root
				}
			}
			random_scope_play_involved = {
				limit = {
					has_war_with = root
				}
				save_scope_as = garibaldi_peasants_foe_scope
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_variable = garibaldi_var
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = garibaldi_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = emboldened_peasantry_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:garibaldi_peasants_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = farmers
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = garibaldi_events.4.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		random_list = {
			80 = {
				scope:garibaldi_scope = {
					exile_character = yes
					clean_up_exile_character_roles = yes
				}
				set_variable = rejected_garibaldi
			}
			20 = {
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = government_petition_events

government_petition_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			var:government_petition_variant = 1
		}
		video = "europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			var:government_petition_variant = 2
		}
		video = "unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			var:government_petition_variant = 3
		}
		video = "asia_politician_parliament_motion"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = government_petition_events.1.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = government_petition_events.1.d
				trigger = {
					has_variable = government_petition_variant
					var:government_petition_variant = 1
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = government_petition_events.1.d2
				trigger = {
					has_variable = government_petition_variant
					var:government_petition_variant = 2
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = government_petition_events.1.d3
				trigger = {
					has_variable = government_petition_variant
					var:government_petition_variant = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = government_petition_events.1.f
				trigger = {
					has_variable = government_petition_variant
					var:government_petition_variant = 1
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = government_petition_events.1.f2
				trigger = {
					has_variable = government_petition_variant
					var:government_petition_variant = 2
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = government_petition_events.1.f3
				trigger = {
					has_variable = government_petition_variant
					var:government_petition_variant = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		#Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
            limit = {
                is_in_government = yes
                ig_counts_as_marginal = no
                any_preferred_law = {
                    law_is_valid_for_ig_petition = yes
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = government_petition_ig
            ordered_preferred_law = {
                limit = {
					law_is_valid_for_ig_petition = yes
                }
                min = 0
                max = 2
                check_range_bounds = no
                save_scope_as = government_petition_law
            }
			ordered_preferred_law = {
                limit = {
					law_is_valid_for_ig_petition = yes
					NOT = {
						scope:government_petition_law ?= THIS
					}
                }
                min = 1
                max = 3
                check_range_bounds = no
                save_scope_as = government_petition_law_2
            }
			set_variable = { name = petitioning_ig days = 100 }
        }
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				set_variable = { name = government_petition_variant value = 1 }
			}
			4 = {
				trigger = {
					NAND = {  # Will not trigger twice in a row
						has_variable = government_petition_variant
						var:government_petition_variant = 2
					}
					scope:government_petition_ig ?= {
						ig_approval < 0
					}
				}
				set_variable = { name = government_petition_variant value = 2 }
			}
			4 = {
				trigger = {
					NAND = { # Will not trigger twice in a row
						has_variable = government_petition_variant
						var:government_petition_variant = 3
					}
					scope:government_petition_ig ?= { # Just in general a competent politician
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_trait = basic_political_operator
								has_trait = experienced_political_operator
								has_trait = expert_political_operator
								has_trait = charismatic
								has_trait = inspirational_orator
								has_trait = firebrand
								has_trait = ambitious
								has_trait = persistent
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_variable = { name = government_petition_variant value = 3 }
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOR = {
			exists = scope:government_petition_ig
			exists = scope:government_petition_law
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = government_petition_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = scope:government_petition_law
            }
            set_variable = { name = desired_law_var value = scope:government_petition_law }
        }
		custom_tooltip = government_petition_tt
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_government_petition
		}
		custom_tooltip = government_petition_active_warning_tt
		show_as_tooltip = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_active_government_petition
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = government_petition_events.1.b
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:government_petition_law_2
		}
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = scope:government_petition_law_2
            }
            set_variable = { name = desired_law_var value = scope:government_petition_law_2 }
        }
		custom_tooltip = government_petition_2_tt
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_government_petition
		}
		custom_tooltip = government_petition_active_warning_tt
		show_as_tooltip = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_active_government_petition
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = government_petition_events.1.c1
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				scope:government_petition_ig = {
					has_variable = government_petition_rejected
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = government_petition_blown_off_tt
		scope:government_petition_ig = {
			set_variable = { name = government_petition_rejected years = 20 }
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_petition_rejected_legitimacy
			days = normal_modifier_time
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		scope:government_petition_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_petition_rejected_ig_opinion
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = government_petition_events.1.c2
		trigger = {
			scope:government_petition_ig = {
				has_variable = government_petition_rejected
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = government_petition_blown_off_2_tt
		scope:government_petition_ig = {
			set_variable = { name = government_petition_rejected years = 20 }
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_petition_rejected_legitimacy
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = 3
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		scope:government_petition_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_petition_rejected_ig_opinion
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 4
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}

namespace = government_collapse

government_collapse.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_courtroom_upheaval"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	title = government_collapse.1.t
	desc = government_collapse.1.d
	flavor = government_collapse.1.f

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		#Triggered by on_action
	}

	cooldown = { days = 91 }

	immediate = {
		random_active_party = {
			save_scope_as = relevant_party
		}
		ordered_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				has_party = yes
				is_in_government = no
				is_insurrectionary = yes
			}
			order_by = ig_clout
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = government_collapse.1.a
		default_option = yes
		random_active_party = {
			disband_party = yes
		}
		scripted_effect_parties_disappearence = yes
		activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
	}
	option = {
		name = government_collapse.1.b
		trigger = {
			exists = currently_enacting_law
			scope:relevant_ig = {
				law_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value < neutral
				}
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_last_minute_compromise
				days = short_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		cancel_enactment = yes
	}
}

historical_agitators.1 = { #Vladimir Lenin
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:historic_agitator_scope

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = historical_agitators.1.t
	desc = historical_agitators.1.d
	flavor = historical_agitators.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		c:RUS ?= this
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			ig_approval < 0
		}
		game_date > 1890.1.1
		has_technology_researched = political_agitation
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
		}
		NOT = { has_global_variable = lenin_spawn }
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = rus_lenin_character_template
			on_created = {
				set_variable = lenin_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lenin_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = lenin_spawn
	}

	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			interest_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_lenin_in_exile
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.1.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_national_guard
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_secret_police
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = rus_lenin_character_template
			}
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_lenin_harassed_by_spies
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		capital = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.1.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = rus_lenin_character_template
			}
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			add_trait = special_character_lenin_popularity
		}
		capital = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
		}
	}
}

historical_agitators.6 = { #Charles Maurras
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:historic_agitator_scope

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	title = historical_agitators.6.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_journal_entry = je_dreyfus_affair
					any_character_in_void = {
						has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
					}
				}
				desc = historical_agitators.6.d1
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_dreyfus_affair }
				}
				desc = historical_agitators.6.d2
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_journal_entry = je_dreyfus_affair
				}
				desc = historical_agitators.6.f1
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_dreyfus_affair }
				}
				desc = historical_agitators.6.f2
			}
		}
	}

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		c:FRA ?= this
		game_date > 1895.1.1
		OR = {
			has_journal_entry = je_dreyfus_affair
			has_variable = dreyfus_trial_reincarceration
			has_variable = dreyfus_trial_lesser_sentence
			has_variable = dreyfus_trial_exoneration
			has_technology_researched = political_agitation
		}
		NOT = { has_global_variable = maurras_spawn }
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = fra_maurras_character_template
			on_created = {
				set_variable = maurras_var
				if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							has_journal_entry = je_divided_monarchists
						}
					}
					set_ideology = ideology:ideology_legitimist
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
						}
					}
					set_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
				}
				else = {
					set_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = maurras_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = maurras_spawn
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_void = {
					has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
				}
			}
			random_character_in_void = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = alfred_dreyfus_var
				}
				save_scope_as = alfred_dreyfus_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.6.a
		default_option = yes
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = fra_maurras_character_template
			}
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			add_trait = special_character_maurras_popularity
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.6.b
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = fra_maurras_character_template
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_maurras_antisemitism
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		capital = {
			add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = {
				religion = rel:jewish
				multiplier = -2
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.6.c
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = fra_maurras_character_template
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_maurras_monarchism
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
			}
			add_loyalists = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = shopkeepers
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_loyalists = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = shopkeepers
			}
		}
	}
}

		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				leader = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_bonapartist
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				add_radicals = {
					value = medium_radicals
					interest_group = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.8.b
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = fra_napoleon_iii_character_template
			}
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			add_trait = special_character_napoleonic_return
		}
	}
}

historical_agitators.9 = { #Norton
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:california_scope

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:historic_agitator_scope

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	title = historical_agitators.9.t
	desc = historical_agitators.9.d
	flavor = historical_agitators.9.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		OR = {
			c:USA ?= this
			c:CAL ?= this
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			state_region = s:STATE_CALIFORNIA
			is_incorporated = yes
		}
		game_date > 1860.1.1
		game_date < 1880.1.1
		NOT = { has_global_variable = emperor_norton_spawn }
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = usa_joshua_norton_character_template
			on_created = {
				set_variable = emperor_norton_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = emperor_norton_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_CALIFORNIA
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = california_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = emperor_norton_spawn
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		scope:historic_agitator_scope ?= {
			is_character_alive = no
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.9.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			add_trait = special_character_emperor_norton_popularity
		}
		scope:california_scope = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.9.b
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
	}
}

	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = historical_agitators.10.t
	desc = historical_agitators.10.d
	flavor = historical_agitators.10.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		c:RUS ?= this
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			ig_approval < 0
		}
		game_date > 1872.1.1
		game_date < 1917.1.1
		has_technology_researched = anarchism
		NOT = { has_global_variable = kropotkin_spawn }
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic }
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = rus_pyotr_kropotkin_character_template
			on_created = {
				set_variable = kropotkin_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = kropotkin_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = kropotkin_spawn
	}

	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.10.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			interest_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_kropotkin_in_exile
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.10.b
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = rus_pyotr_kropotkin_character_template
			}
		}
		capital = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.10.c
		trigger = {
			has_global_variable = trans_siberian_done
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = rus_pyotr_kropotkin_character_template
			}
		}
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_west_siberia
			}
			order_by = state_population
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
		}
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_east_siberia
			}
			order_by = state_population
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
		}
	}

			base = 25
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
						ig_approval < -5
					}
				}
				add = -15
			}
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = fra_georges_boulanger_character_template
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_fired_boulanger
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = large_radicals
			interest_group = ig:ig_armed_forces
		}
	}
}

historical_agitators.18 = { #Lassalle
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:historic_agitator_scope

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	title = historical_agitators.18.t
	desc = historical_agitators.18.d
	flavor = historical_agitators.18.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_german
		game_date > 1848.1.1
		game_date < 1885.1.1
		has_technology_researched = dialectics
		NOT = { has_global_variable = lassalle_spawn }
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic }
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = pru_ferdinand_lassalle_character_template
			on_created = {
				set_variable = lassalle_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lassalle_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = lassalle_spawn
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			exists = scope:historic_agitator_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.18.a
		default_option = yes
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = pru_ferdinand_lassalle_character_template
			}
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope ?= {
			add_trait = special_character_agent_of_the_sovereign
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.18.b
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = pru_ferdinand_lassalle_character_template
			}
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope ?= {
			add_trait = special_character_agent_of_the_sovereign
			interest_group = {
				add_modifier ={
					name = modifier_agent_of_the_sovereign_ig
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

historical_agitators.20 = { #Hugo
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:historic_agitator_scope

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	title = historical_agitators.20.t
	desc = historical_agitators.20.d
	flavor = historical_agitators.20.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		c:FRA ?= this
		game_date < 1885.1.1
		ruler = {
			OR = {
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_autocracy
					value > neutral
				}
				AND = {
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_autocracy
						value > strongly_approve # Equals 'count'
					}
					interest_group = {
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_autocracy
							value > neutral
						}
						NOT = {
							law_stance = {
								law = law_type:law_autocracy
								value > strongly_approve # Equals 'count'
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_global_variable = victor_hugo_spawn }
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = fra_victor_hugo_character_template
			on_created = {
				set_variable = victor_hugo_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = victor_hugo_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = victor_hugo_spawn
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = ruler_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.20.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			exile_character = yes
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = ig:ig_intelligentsia
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.20.b
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = fra_victor_hugo_character_template
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			interest_group = scope:historic_agitator_scope.interest_group
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.20.c
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = imprison_hugo_tt
		add_radicals = {
			value = very_large_radicals
			interest_group = ig:ig_intelligentsia
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
		}
	}
}
