﻿namespace = land_ownership_law_events

# Debate Event - The SCOPE Homestead Act
land_ownership_law_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.1.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.1.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.1.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_monarch_holding_court"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
		}
		NOR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_oligarchy
		}
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = no
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			law_stance = {
				law = law_type:law_homesteading
				value < neutral
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_homestead_acts
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = no
			}
			save_scope_as = homesteading_act_state_scope
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = landowners_scope
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			save_scope_as = rural_folks_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:homesteading_act_state_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = homestead_act_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_homestead_acts
				sponsor = scope:rural_folks_scope
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = homestead_favouring_farmers_neg
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = homestead_favouring_farmers_pos
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.1.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = surge_of_popular_support_modifier
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_homestead_acts
				sponsor = scope:rural_folks_scope
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = aristocrats
			value = small_radicals
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.1.c
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = bowing_to_the_plantocracy
		}
		scope:homesteading_act_state_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = planter_dominance_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = homestead_favouring_planters_pos
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = homestead_favouring_planters_neg
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Advancement event - The Shackled Soul of the Nation
land_ownership_law_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.2.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.2.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.2.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_realist_household"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		has_technology_researched = romanticism
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = landowners_scope
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			save_scope_as = devout_scope
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			save_scope_as = rural_folks_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = dissenter_support_for_law
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = cultural_custodians_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.2.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = dissenter_support_for_law
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = internal_discord_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = internal_discord_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Stall event - An Acre too Far
land_ownership_law_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.3.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.3.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.3.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_courtroom_upheaval"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
		any_interest_group = {
			is_in_government = yes
			leader = {
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			leader = {
				NOR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
			scope:anti_kulak_leaders_scope = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:anti_kulak_leaders_scope
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
				leader = {
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
					}
				}
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = anti_kulak_leaders_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = anti_kulak_ig_scope
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = rural_folks_leader_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = rural_folks_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rural_hysteria_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = concise_collectivization_program
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = peasants
			value = medium_radicals
		}
		scope:anti_kulak_leaders_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trait = inspirational_orator
				}
				remove_trait = inspirational_orator
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = demagogue
					}
					NOT = {
						has_trait = firebrand
					}
				}
				add_trait = demagogue
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.3.b
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rural_hysteria_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = peasants
			value = small_radicals
		}
		scope:anti_kulak_leaders_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = cautious }
				}
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						add_trait = cautious
					}
					50 = {
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.3.c
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rural_paranoia_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = confused_collectivization_program
		}
		scope:anti_kulak_leaders_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = cautious }
				}
				add_trait = cautious
			}
		}
	}
}

# Debate Event - The Plight of the Second Sons
land_ownership_law_events.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.4.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.4.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.4.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_prosperous_farm"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_homesteading
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_tenant_farmers
		}
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_type = building_rye_farm
					is_building_type = building_wheat_farm
					is_building_type = building_rice_farm
					is_building_type = building_maize_farm
					is_building_type = building_millet_farm
					is_building_type = building_livestock_ranch
				}
				occupancy > 0.8
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		OR = {
			ig:ig_landowners ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
			ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = landowners_scope
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			save_scope_as = industrialists_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_building = {
					OR = {
						is_building_type = building_rye_farm
						is_building_type = building_wheat_farm
						is_building_type = building_rice_farm
						is_building_type = building_maize_farm
						is_building_type = building_millet_farm
						is_building_type = building_livestock_ranch
					}
					occupancy > 0.8
				}
			}
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_building_type = building_rye_farm
						is_building_type = building_wheat_farm
						is_building_type = building_rice_farm
						is_building_type = building_maize_farm
						is_building_type = building_millet_farm
						is_building_type = building_livestock_ranch
						occupancy > 0.8
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = commercialized_agriculture_building_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = commercialized_agriculture_state_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = for_the_nations_second_sons_modifier
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.4.b
		trigger = {
			ig:ig_landowners ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = farmland_consolidation_modifier_pos
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = farmland_consolidation_modifier_neg
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = aristocrats
			value = small_radicals
		}
		scope:commercialized_agriculture_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = peasants
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.4.c
		trigger = {
			ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = farmland_consolidation_modifier_pos
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = farmland_consolidation_modifier_neg
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = capitalists
			value = small_radicals
		}
		scope:commercialized_agriculture_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = peasants
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

# Advance Event - Farmer Cooperatives
land_ownership_law_events.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:farmer_coops_state_scope

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.5.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.5.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.5.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_prosperous_farm"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_tenant_farmers
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
		}
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_farmers_cooperatives
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = farmer_coops_state_scope
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			save_scope_as = rf_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.5.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = co_operatively_backed
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.5.b
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_farmers_cooperatives
				sponsor = scope:rf_ig
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
		scope:farmer_coops_state_scope = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = peasants
				value = medium_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = laborers
				value = small_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = shopkeepers
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.5.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = the_cooperative_movement
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = the_cooperative_movement
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:farmer_coops_state_scope = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = peasants
				value = small_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = laborers
				value = medium_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = shopkeepers
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

# Stall Event - Land From the Tiller
land_ownership_law_events.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.6.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.6.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.6.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_russian_serfs"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom }
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = landowners_scope
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			save_scope_as = rural_folks_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.6.a
		default_option = yes
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = rural_hysteria_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = estates_claims_backed_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = peasants
			value = medium_radicals
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = aristocrats
			value = medium_radicals
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.6.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = limited_land_redistribution_proposal
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = peasants
			value = small_radicals
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = aristocrats
			value = small_radicals
		}
	}
}

# Stall Event - Enthrallment in an Enligthened Age
land_ownership_law_events.9 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.9.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.9.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.9.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_public_protest"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_serfdom
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			law_stance = {
				law = law_type:law_serfdom
				value <= disapprove
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_pop = {
					is_pop_type = farmers
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = serfdom_enactment_state_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.9.a
		default_option = yes
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = the_return_of_the_good_old_days
			}
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_serfdom
					value <= disapprove
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = anti_feudal_opposition
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = aristocrats
			value = small_radicals
		}
		scope:serfdom_enactment_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = farmers
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.9.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = legal_jargon_complicates_reform
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_serfdom
					value <= disapprove
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = anti_feudal_opposition
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:serfdom_enactment_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = farmers
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

land_ownership_law_events.10 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:kulaks_state

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.10.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.10.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.10.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_fire"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
		any_scope_state = {
			turmoil > 0.15
		}
		OR = {
			ig:ig_landowners ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
					value < approve
				}
			}
			ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
					value < approve
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
					is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
				}
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
					value < approve
				}
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
			save_scope_as = landowners_ig
		}
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				turmoil > 0.15
			}
			order_by = state_population
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = kulaks_state
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.10.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_collectivisation_protests
		}
		scope:kulaks_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_destruction_of_grain
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
				strata = upper
			}
			every_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = root
					turmoil > 0.15
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_destruction_of_grain
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.10.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_collectivisation_protests_capitulated
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
					value > neutral
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_collectivisation_protests_capitulated_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:kulaks_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_destruction_of_grain
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			every_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = root
					turmoil > 0.15
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_destruction_of_grain
					days = normal_modifier_time
					multiplier = 0.5
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

land_ownership_law_events.11 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.11.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.11.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.11.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_union_leader"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			ig_approval < 5
		}
		NOT = {
			country_has_state_religion = rel:animist
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_pop = {
			limit = {
				has_state_religion = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = majority_religion
		}
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = ruler_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.11.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivization_progress
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					leader = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
					}
					leader = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat
					}
					is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivisation
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			pop_type = clergymen
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			pop_type = peasants
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.11.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivization_progress
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_capitulated_to_religious_agitation
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.11.c
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivization_progress
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_burned_religious_pamphlets
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			pop_type = clergymen
		}
	}
}

land_ownership_law_events.12 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:collectivised_state

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.12.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.12.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.12.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "asia_factory_accident"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			ig_approval > 5
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			order_by = state_population
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = collectivised_state
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = rural_folk_leader
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.12.a
		default_option = yes
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_state_planners_upset
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_state_planners_overridden
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_state_planning_kerfluffle
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.12.b
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_state_planners_basically_acceptable
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:collectivised_state = {
			start_building_construction = building_steel_mill
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_backyard_steel_progress
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.12.c
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:rural_folk_leader
		}
		random_list = {
			70 = {
				ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
					leader = {
						add_modifier = {
							name = modifier_state_planning_pop_up
							days = long_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = modifier_rural_state_planning_go_ahead
				}
			}
			30 = {
				ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
					leader = {
						add_modifier = {
							name = modifier_state_planning_pop_down
							days = long_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = modifier_rural_state_planning_delay
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

land_ownership_law_events.15 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = land_ownership_law_events.15.t
	desc = land_ownership_law_events.15.d
	flavor = land_ownership_law_events.15.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_leader_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture
		NOT = {
			is_subject = yes
		}
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_isolationism
		}
		any_neighbouring_state = {
			NOT = {
				owner = {
					this = root
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				OR = {
					has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_extraterritoriality_rights
					has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_open_legal_questions
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:current_law_scope
			}
			NOT = {
				is_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope.type
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_neighbouring_state = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					owner = {
						this = root
					}
				}
			}
			owner = {
				save_scope_as = neighbour_country
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			save_scope_as = industrialists_ig
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = landowners_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.15.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = { country = scope:neighbour_country value = -10 }
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_legal_extraterrioriality_concerns_blocked
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.15.b
		change_relations = { country = scope:neighbour_country value = 10 }
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_legal_extraterrioriality_concerns
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_extraterritoriality_rights
				sponsor = scope:industrialists_ig
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.15.c
		change_relations = { country = scope:neighbour_country value = 10 }
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_overbearing_caution
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_open_legal_questions
				sponsor = scope:industrialists_ig # Nobody really supports this, but amendments need a sponsor
				cooldown = 60
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			strata = lower
		}
	}
}

		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = landowners_ig
		}
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = capital_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.18.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_peasantry_rebranded
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.18.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_peasantry_rebranded_landlords
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = land_ownership_law_events.18.c
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_peasantry_rebranded_concessions
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			strata = lower
		}
	}
}

# Advance Event - Darwin Among the Machines
luddite_law_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_gears_pistons"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = luddite_law_events.1.t
	desc = luddite_law_events.1.d
	flavor = luddite_law_events.1.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_industry_banned
		year >= 1859 # Origin of the Species
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = machine_men_with_machine_minds_modifier
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.1.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = time_to_go_outside_modifier
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.1.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				leader = {
					NOT = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_luddite
					}
					OR = {
						has_trait = psychological_affliction
						has_trait = alcoholic
						has_trait = cocaine_addiction
						has_trait = opium_addiction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			leader = {
				set_ideology = ideology:ideology_luddite
			}
		}
	}
}

# Debate Event - Simple Living
luddite_law_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = luddite_law_events.2.t
	desc = luddite_law_events.2.d
	flavor = luddite_law_events.2.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_industry_banned
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			NOT = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
			}
			leader = {
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat
					AND = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_luddite
						has_trait = pious
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_primary_culture = {
			save_scope_as = simple_living_primary_culture_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				NOT = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
				}
				leader = {
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat
						AND = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_luddite
							has_trait = pious
						}
					}
				}
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = simple_living_leader_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = simple_living_ig_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = zealous_advocates_modifier
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = widespread_dogmatic_intolerance
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:simple_living_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = a_zealot_endorsed_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.2.b
		scope:simple_living_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = a_zealot_scorned_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Stall Event - Furor Technophobicus
luddite_law_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:technophobic_fury_state_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_vandalized_storefront"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = luddite_law_events.3.t
	desc = luddite_law_events.3.d
	flavor = luddite_law_events.3.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_industry_banned
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_group = bg_light_industry
					is_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
				}
				occupancy >= 0.25
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				is_pop_type = machinists
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_building = {
					OR = {
						is_building_group = bg_light_industry
						is_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
					}
					occupancy >= 0.25
				}
				any_scope_pop = {
					is_pop_type = machinists
				}
			}
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_building_group = bg_light_industry
						is_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
					}
					occupancy >= 0.25
				}
				save_scope_as = technophobic_fury_building_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = technophobic_fury_state_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = a_reputation_for_violence_modifier
		}
		scope:technophobic_fury_building_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rage_against_the_machine_modifier
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.3.b
		scope:technophobic_fury_building_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rage_against_the_machine_modifier
				days = short_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = yes
				}
			}
			ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = anti_worker_crackdowns_modifier
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		scope:technophobic_fury_state_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = machinists
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = laborers
				value = large_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

luddite_law_events.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:class_collaboration_state
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_cops_march"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = luddite_law_events.6.t
	desc = luddite_law_events.6.d
	flavor = luddite_law_events.6.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_industry_banned
		any_scope_state = {
			has_building = building_urban_center
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			NOT = {
				ig_is_socialist = yes
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_building = building_urban_center
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			order_by = b:building_urban_center.level
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = class_collaboration_state
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			save_scope_as = industrialists_ig
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.6.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_collective_antagonism_a
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.6.b
		random_list = {
			40 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = modifier_collective_antagonism_b1
				}
			}
			40 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = modifier_collective_antagonism_b2
				}
			}
			20 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = modifier_collective_antagonism_b3
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = luddite_law_events.6.c
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_collective_antagonism_funds
			months = 12
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_collective_antagonism_c
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = natural_borders

# start event
natural_borders.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = natural_borders.1.t
	desc = natural_borders.1.d
	flavor = natural_borders.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_signed_contract"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_french_natural_borders
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_powerful = yes
				is_in_government = yes
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_jingoist_leader
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ethno_nationalist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_authoritarian
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_bonapartist
						}
					}
				}
			}
			leader = { save_scope_as = borders_leader }
		}
		s:STATE_SAVOY = {
			random_scope_state = {
				save_scope_as = savoy_state
			}
		}
		s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE = {
			random_scope_state = {
				save_scope_as = rhineland_state
			}
		}
		s:STATE_FLANDERS = {
			random_scope_state = {
				save_scope_as = flanders_state
			}
		}
		c:FRA ?= { save_scope_as = france_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = natural_borders.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { owns_entire_state_region = STATE_WALLONIA }
			}
			s:STATE_WALLONIA = {
				add_claim = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { owns_entire_state_region = STATE_FLANDERS }
			}
			s:STATE_FLANDERS = {
				add_claim = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { owns_entire_state_region = STATE_RHINELAND }
			}
			s:STATE_RHINELAND = {
				add_claim = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTH_RHINE }
			}
			s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE = {
				add_claim = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SAVOY }
			}
			s:STATE_SAVOY = {
				add_claim = ROOT
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = france_natural_borders_pursuit
			days = very_long_modifier_time
			# country_infamy_decay_mult
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
						state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
						state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
						state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
						state_region = s:STATE_SAVOY
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_event = { id = natural_borders.4 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_diplomatic_relevance = c:FRA
					NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
				}
				post_notification = fra_natural_borders_notification
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # imperialist IG's unhappy
		name = natural_borders.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = dismiss_natural_borders_tt
			set_variable = reject_natural_borders
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_powerful = yes
				is_in_government = yes
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_jingoist_leader
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ethno_nationalist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_authoritarian
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_bonapartist
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = no_natural_borders
				days = long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
				# interest_group_approval_add
			}
		}
	}
}

# France annexes Savoy
natural_borders.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:savoy_state
	title = natural_borders.3.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = natural_borders.3.d
				trigger = {
					exists = scope:nice_state
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = natural_borders.3.d2
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = natural_borders.3.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_signed_contract"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	trigger = {
		# triggered by france_annex_savoy
	}

	immediate = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				is_at_war = no
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_SAVOY
					is_split_state = no
					is_capital = no
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = savoy_country
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = savoy_ruler
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_SAVOY
					is_split_state = no
					is_capital = no
				}
				save_scope_as = savoy_state
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_PROVENCE
					}
				}
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						state_region = s:STATE_PROVENCE
					}
					save_scope_as = nice_state
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # incorporate Savoy into France
		name = natural_borders.3.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_owed_obligation_by = scope:savoy_country
			}
			scope:savoy_country = {
				set_owes_obligation_to = {
					country = ROOT
					setting = no
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_variable = fra_savoy_sardinia
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				set_owes_obligation_to = {
					country = scope:savoy_country
					setting = yes
				}
			}
		}
		scope:savoy_state = {
			set_state_owner = ROOT
			set_state_type = incorporated
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:nice_state
			}
			scope:nice_state = {
				set_state_owner = ROOT
			}
		}
		s:STATE_SAVOY = {
			remove_homeland = cu:north_italian
		}
	}

	option = { # conduct a plebiscite
		name = natural_borders.3.b
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_owed_obligation_by = scope:savoy_country
			}
			scope:savoy_country = {
				set_owes_obligation_to = {
					country = ROOT
					setting = no
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_variable = fra_savoy_sardinia
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				set_owes_obligation_to = {
					country = scope:savoy_country
					setting = yes
				}
			}
		}
		scope:savoy_state = {
			set_state_owner = ROOT
			set_state_type = unincorporated
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:french
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:francoprovencal
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:nice_state
			}
			scope:nice_state = {
				set_state_owner = ROOT
			}
		}
		s:STATE_SAVOY = {
			remove_homeland = cu:north_italian
		}
	}
}

# Notification event
natural_borders.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:french_claim_state
	title = natural_borders.4.t
	desc = natural_borders.4.d
	flavor = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = natural_borders.4.f
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
								state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = natural_borders.4.f2
				trigger = {
					any_scope_state = {
						OR = {
							state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
							state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"

	trigger = {
		# triggered by je_french_natural_borders
	}

	immediate = {
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = france_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
					state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
					state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
					state_region = s:STATE_SAVOY
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = french_claim_state
		}
	}

	option = { # Prepare for war
		name = natural_borders.4.a
		default_option = yes
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
					state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
					state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
					state_region = s:STATE_SAVOY
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = rally_against_france
				days = long_modifier_time
				# state_conscription_rate_mult
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 19
		}
	}

	option = { # Try to appease France
		name = natural_borders.4.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:FRA
			value = 20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

namespace = rhine_confederation

rhine_confederation.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = rhine_confederation.1.t
	desc = rhine_confederation.1.d
	flavor = rhine_confederation.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_signed_contract"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					capital = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_rhine
							region = sr:region_north_germany
							region = sr:region_south_germany
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = ROOT
							type = personal_union
							is_initiator = no
						}
					}
					is_player = yes
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					capital = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_rhine
							region = sr:region_north_germany
							region = sr:region_south_germany
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = ROOT
							type = personal_union
							is_initiator = no
						}
					}
					is_player = yes
				}
				change_tag = RHN
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					capital = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_rhine
							region = sr:region_north_germany
							region = sr:region_south_germany
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = ROOT
							type = personal_union
							is_initiator = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ordered_country = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					capital = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_rhine
							region = sr:region_north_germany
							region = sr:region_south_germany
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = ROOT
							type = personal_union
							is_initiator = no
						}
					}
				}
				order_by = country_rank
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				change_tag = RHN
			}
		}
		else = {
			ordered_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = {
						OR = {
							is_homeland = cu:south_german
							is_homeland = cu:north_german
						}
					}
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_rhine
						region = sr:region_north_germany
						region = sr:region_south_germany
					}
					is_incorporated = no
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							ROOT = {
								country_has_primary_culture = cu:french
							}
						}
						NOR = {
							state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
							state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
							state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
							state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
						}
					}
				}
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				save_scope_as = rhine_state
			}
			create_country = {
				tag = RHN
				origin = ROOT
				state = scope:rhine_state
			}
			ROOT = {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = c:RHN
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		c:RHN ?= {
			save_scope_as = rhine_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = rhine_confederation.1.a
		trigger = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:french
			any_state = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
					state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
					state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
				}
				NOT = {
					owner = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					c:RHN ?= THIS
				}
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				capital = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_rhine
						region = sr:region_north_germany
						region = sr:region_south_germany
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = ROOT
						type = personal_union
						is_initiator = no
					}
				}
			}
			c:RHN ?= {
				annex_with_incorporation = prev
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = {
					OR = {
						is_homeland = cu:south_german
						is_homeland = cu:north_german
					}
				}
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_rhine
					region = sr:region_north_germany
					region = sr:region_south_germany
				}
				NOR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
					state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
					state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
				}
				is_incorporated = no
			}
			set_state_owner = c:RHN
			set_state_type = incorporated
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_RHINELAND
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_RHINE
					state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
					state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
				}
				c:RHN ?= owner
			}
			set_state_owner = root
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = rhine_confederation.1.b
		default_option = yes
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					c:RHN ?= THIS
				}
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				capital = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_rhine
						region = sr:region_north_germany
						region = sr:region_south_germany
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = ROOT
						type = personal_union
						is_initiator = no
					}
				}
			}
			c:RHN ?= {
				annex_with_incorporation = prev
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = {
					OR = {
						is_homeland = cu:south_german
						is_homeland = cu:north_german
					}
				}
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_rhine
					region = sr:region_north_germany
					region = sr:region_south_germany
				}
				is_incorporated = no
			}
			set_state_owner = c:RHN
			set_state_type = incorporated
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = paris_commune

paris_commune.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_springtime_of_nations"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"

	title = paris_commune.1.t
	desc = paris_commune.1.d
	flavor = paris_commune.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		#Triggered by JE
	}

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = paris_commune_happened
		set_variable = paris_commune_spawn
		create_country = {
			tag = PRC
			origin = this
			province = p:x71DA2E
			on_created = {
				s:STATE_ILE_DE_FRANCE = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:PRC
						provinces = { x906000 }
					}
				}
				change_relations = {
					country = c:FRA
					value = -50
				}
				set_immune_to_revolutions = yes
				set_ruling_interest_groups = { ig_intelligentsia ig_trade_unions ig_armed_forces }
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
					set_interest_group_name = ig_national_guard
					remove_ideology = ideology_loyalist
					add_ideology = ideology_proletarian
					remove_ideology = ideology_patriotic
					add_ideology = ideology_republican
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_elected_bureaucrats
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_elected_bureaucrats
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_women_in_the_workplace
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_women_in_the_workplace
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_total_separation
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_total_separation
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_mass_conscription
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_mass_conscription
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_public_schools
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_public_schools
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_interventionism
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_interventionism
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_national_guard
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_national_guard
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_right_of_assembly
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_right_of_assembly
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_closed_borders
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_closed_borders
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_factory_councils
						}
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_factory_councils
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_paris_commune_politics
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_paris_commune_not_dying_economy
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
				create_paris_commune_characters = yes
				call_election = {
					months = 1
				}
			}
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			save_scope_as = paris_commune_scope
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
				save_scope_as = national_guard_scope
			}
			create_bidirectional_truce = {
				country = c:FRA
				months = 24
			}
		}
		set_variable = { name = PRC_recognition_level value = 0 }
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					c:PRC ?= THIS
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = paris_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				socialist_revolution_ongoing = yes
			}
			c:PRC ?= {
				activate_law = law_type:law_council_republic
			}
		}
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = ruler_scope
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_diplomatic_relevance = ROOT
				country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				NOT = { ROOT = THIS }
			}
			trigger_event = { id = paris_commune.5 days = 14 }
		}
		ROOT = {
			save_scope_as = france_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = paris_commune.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_the_paris_commune_france
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_journal_entry = {
					type = je_the_paris_commune_communards
				}
				add_loyalists = {
					value = 0.9
				}
				c:FRA ?= {
					add_treasury = -100000
				}
				add_treasury = 100000
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_global_variable = { name = paris_commune_progress_var value = 40 }
			set_global_variable = { name = paris_commune_progress_drift value = -1 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = commune_progress_start_at_40
		set_variable = paris_commune_fra_perspective
	}
	option = {
		name = paris_commune.1.b
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_the_paris_commune_france
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_journal_entry = {
					type = je_the_paris_commune_communards
				}
				add_loyalists = {
					value = 0.9
				}
				c:FRA ?= {
					add_treasury = -100000
				}
				add_treasury = 100000
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_global_variable = { name = paris_commune_progress_var value = 60 }
			set_global_variable = { name = paris_commune_progress_drift value = 1 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = commune_progress_start_at_60
		set_variable = paris_commune_prc_perspective
	}
}

paris_commune.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_cops_march"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	title = paris_commune.2.t
	desc = paris_commune.2.d
	flavor = paris_commune.2.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = paris_commune_crushed_var
		c:PRC ?= {
			save_scope_as = paris_commune_scope
		}
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					c:PRC ?= THIS
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = paris_scope
		}
		random_military_formation = {
			limit = {
				any_combat_unit = {
					building.state.region = {
						this = sr:region_france
					}
				}
				any_scope_general = {
					has_role = general
				}
			}
			random_scope_general = {
				save_scope_as = commune_destroyer_general
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = paris_commune.2.a
		default_option = yes
		c:FRA ?= {
			annex_as_civil_war = c:PRC
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ILE_DE_FRANCE
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.02
				pop_type = soldiers
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.02
				pop_type = academics
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.02
				pop_type = laborers
			}
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 30
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_repression_of_commune
			days = long_modifier_time
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_void = {
					has_variable = imprisoned_general_var
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					random_character_in_void = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = imprisoned_general_var
						}
						free_character_from_void = yes
					}
				}
				4 = {
					random_character_in_void = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = imprisoned_general_var
						}
						kill_character = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = paris_commune.2.b
		c:FRA ?= {
			annex_as_civil_war = c:PRC
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ILE_DE_FRANCE
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.01
				pop_type = soldiers
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.01
				pop_type = academics
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.01
				pop_type = laborers
			}
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_void = {
					has_variable = imprisoned_general_var
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					random_character_in_void = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = imprisoned_general_var
						}
						free_character_from_void = yes
					}
				}
				2 = {
					random_character_in_void = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = imprisoned_general_var
						}
						kill_character = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

paris_commune.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_military_parade"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	title = paris_commune.3.t
	desc = paris_commune.3.d
	flavor = paris_commune.3.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_variable = march_on_versailles }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = march_on_versailles
		c:PRC ?= {
			save_scope_as = paris_commune_scope
		}
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					c:PRC ?= THIS
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = paris_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = paris_commune.3.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = communards_seize_power_tt
			every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					this.interest_group ?= {
						is_in_government = yes
					}
				}
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
			c:PRC ?= {
				every_scope_character = {
					transfer_character = c:FRA
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_role = politician
						}
						set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
					set_interest_group_name = ig_national_guard
					remove_ideology = ideology_loyalist
					add_ideology = ideology_proletarian
					remove_ideology = ideology_patriotic
					add_ideology = ideology_republican
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				remove_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
			ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
			ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
			copy_laws = c:PRC
			annex = c:PRC
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = small_radicals
			strata = lower
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = paris_commune.3.b
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = communards_seize_power_tt
			every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					this.interest_group ?= {
						is_in_government = yes
					}
				}
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
			c:PRC ?= {
				every_scope_character = {
					transfer_character = c:FRA
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_role = politician
						}
						set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
					set_interest_group_name = ig_national_guard
					remove_ideology = ideology_loyalist
					add_ideology = ideology_proletarian
					remove_ideology = ideology_patriotic
					add_ideology = ideology_republican
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				remove_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
			ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
			ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
			copy_laws = c:PRC
			annex = c:PRC
			add_loyalists = {
				value = small_radicals
				strata = lower
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = large_radicals
			pop_type = academics
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = large_radicals
			pop_type = laborers
		}
	}
}

paris_commune.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_springtime_of_nations"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	title = paris_commune.5.t
	desc = paris_commune.5.d
	flavor = paris_commune.5.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		#Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = france_scope
		}
		ROOT = {
			save_scope_as = recogniser_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = paris_commune.5.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:france_scope.relations:root >= relations_threshold:cordial
				}
				add = 50
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:france_scope
			value = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = paris_commune.5.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:france_scope.relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor
				}
				add = 30
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
				}
				add = 250
			}
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					scope:france_scope = {
						has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
						NOR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
						}
					}
					NOR = {
						has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
					}
					OR = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
				}
				add = 10
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:france_scope
			value = -25
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
				}
			}
			c:FRA ?= {
				change_variable = { name = PRC_recognition_level add = 1 }  # Variable exists only for display purposes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				}
			}
			c:FRA ?= {
				change_variable = { name = PRC_recognition_level add = 0.5 } # Variable exists only for display purposes
			}
		}
		scope:france_scope = {
			post_notification = foreign_country_supports_commune
		}
		custom_tooltip = will_improve_commune_progress_tt
	}
	option = {
		name = paris_commune.5.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:france_scope
			value = -5
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = paris_commune_pulse_events

# 1 - Seizing Church and State
paris_commune_pulse_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:paris_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_christianity"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	title = paris_commune_pulse_events.1.t
	desc = paris_commune_pulse_events.1.d
	flavor = paris_commune_pulse_events.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_variable = paris_commune_prc_perspective
		c:PRC ?= {
			any_interest_group = {
				leader = {
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
					}
				}
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {

		c:PRC ?= {
			save_scope_as = paris_commune_nation_scope
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = seize_church_ig_scope
			}
			capital = {
				save_scope_as = paris_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Close the church until the commune is better established
		name = paris_commune_pulse_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commune_progress_add_10
	        change_global_variable = {
	            name = paris_commune_progress_var
	            add = 10
	        }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = communard_effects_header_tt
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
						}
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = divine_forclosure_modifier
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				convert_population = {
					target = rel:atheist
					value = 0.1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Find all the gold and silver.
		name = paris_commune_pulse_events.1.b
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commune_progress_add_10
	        change_global_variable = {
	            name = paris_commune_progress_var
	            add = 10
	        }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = communard_effects_header_tt
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = the_riches_of_the_church_modifier
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
						}
					}
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = capital_gains_modifier
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Turn it over to [random_faction] for their meetings.
		name = paris_commune_pulse_events.1.c
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commune_progress_add_5
	        change_global_variable = {
	            name = paris_commune_progress_var
	            add = 5
	        }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = communard_effects_header_tt
		}
		scope:seize_church_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = secret_clubhouse_modifier
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			create_character = {
				is_agitator = yes
				religion = rel:atheist
				culture = cu:french
				interest_group = scope:seize_church_ig_scope
				ideology = ideology_nihilist
			}
		}
	}
}

# 2 - Forty Warning Shots
paris_commune_pulse_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:paris_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_prison"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	title = paris_commune_pulse_events.2.t
	desc = paris_commune_pulse_events.2.d
	flavor = paris_commune_pulse_events.2.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_variable = paris_commune_prc_perspective
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = general
			is_busy = no
			is_heir = no
			is_ruler = no
		#	hq = region_france
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			any_interest_group = {
				leader = {
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		c:PRC ?= {
			save_scope_as = paris_commune_nation_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ILE_DE_FRANCE
				}
				save_scope_as = paris_scope
			}
		}

		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				is_busy = no
				is_heir = no
				is_ruler = no
			#	hq = region_france
			}
			save_scope_as = executed_general_scope
		}
	}

	option = { # They did their duty by killing an enemy general.
		name = paris_commune_pulse_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commune_progress_add_10
	        change_global_variable = {
	            name = paris_commune_progress_var
	            add = 10
	        }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = paris_commune_executes_general
			scope:executed_general_scope = {
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		c:PRC ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_interest_group = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
							}
						}
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = communard_effects_header_tt
				}
				every_interest_group = {
					limit = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
							}
						}
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
					add_modifier = {
						name = one_hydra_head_claimed_modifier
						days = short_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Conduct a fair trial. We are not barbarians.
		name = paris_commune_pulse_events.2.b
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commune_progress_add_5
	        change_global_variable = {
	            name = paris_commune_progress_var
	            add = 5
	        }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = paris_commune_pulse_events.2.tt
			scope:executed_general_scope = {
				set_variable = imprisoned_general_var
				add_modifier = {
					name = miraculous_survival_modifier
				}
				place_character_in_void = 10000
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:PRC ?= {
					any_interest_group = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_moderate
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_reformer
							}
						}
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = communard_effects_header_tt
			}
			c:PRC ?= {
				every_interest_group = {
					limit = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_moderate
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_reformer
							}
						}
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
					add_modifier = {
						name = airs_of_civility_modifier
						days = short_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Put them in the Enfants Perdus for their bravery.
		name = paris_commune_pulse_events.2.c
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = commune_progress_subtract_5
	        change_global_variable = {
	            name = paris_commune_progress_var
	            add = -5
	        }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = paris_commune_executes_general
			scope:executed_general_scope = {
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:PRC ?= {
					any_interest_group = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_jingoist_leader
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_authoritarian
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
							}
						}
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = communard_effects_header_tt
			}
			c:PRC ?= {
				every_interest_group = {
					limit = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_jingoist_leader
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_authoritarian
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
							}
						}
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
					add_modifier = {
						name = war_is_the_answer_modifier
						days = short_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
