					is_civil_war_type = revolution
					civil_war_progress_high = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_civil_war = {
			limit = {
				is_civil_war_type = revolution
				civil_war_progress_high = yes
			}
			civil_war_save_scopes = yes
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig_leader
			}
		}
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				has_building = building_barrack
			}
			order_by = state_population
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = shall_not_be_infringed_state
			owner = {
				save_scope_as = agitator_country_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse_events.38.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_medium
		}
		scope:shall_not_be_infringed_state = {
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_unp_barracks_malus
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces.leader = {
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					add_trait = cautious
				}
				33 = {
					add_trait = wrathful
				}
				34 = {
					add_trait = wounded
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse_events.38.b
		trigger = {
			empty_agitator_slots >= 1
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_medium
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			pop_type = laborers
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = agitator_spawn_tt
			create_character = {
				is_agitator = yes
				interest_group = ig_trade_unions
				traits = {
					social_bandit
					popular_commander
				}
			}
		}
		scope:shall_not_be_infringed_state = {
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.02
				pop_type = soldiers
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse_events.38.c
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_small
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_militarised_police_force
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:shall_not_be_infringed_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				strata = lower
			}
		}
	}
}

revolution_pulse_events.39 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = revolution_pulse_events.39.t
	desc = revolution_pulse_events.39.d
	flavor = revolution_pulse_events.39.f

	category = revolution

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_civil_war = {
			is_civil_war_type = revolution
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			is_in_government = no
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			is_insurrectionary = no
		}
		any_interest_group = { is_insurrectionary = yes }
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { any_revolution_ongoing = yes }
			NOT = { any_interest_group = { is_insurrectionary = yes } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_civil_war = {
			limit = {
				is_civil_war_type = revolution
			}
			civil_war_save_scopes = yes
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_insurrectionary = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = revolting_ig
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = no
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				is_insurrectionary = no
			}
			save_scope_as = defecting_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse_events.39.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:revolting_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_salvaged_some_support
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:defecting_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_defected_to_revolutionaries
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse_events.39.b
		scope:revolting_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_salvaged_some_support
				days = short_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}
		scope:defecting_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_coaxing_defectors_back
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_coaxing_defectors
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

﻿namespace = revolution_pulse2_events

revolution_pulse2_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = revolution_pulse2_events.1.t
	desc = revolution_pulse2_events.1.d
	flavor = revolution_pulse2_events.1.f

	category = revolution

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_garibaldi"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		 any_civil_war = {
			civil_war_is_reactionary_revolution = yes
			civil_war_progress_low = yes
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			is_insurrectionary = yes
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			OR = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
				leader = { has_role = general }
			}
		}
		any_country = {
			NOT = { this = ROOT }
			NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
			NOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized }
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
			relations:root >= relations_threshold:neutral
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_civil_war = {
				civil_war_is_reactionary_revolution = yes
				civil_war_progress_low = yes
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_civil_war = {
			limit = {
				civil_war_is_reactionary_revolution = yes
				civil_war_progress_low = yes
			}
			civil_war_save_scopes = yes
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_insurrectionary = yes
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
					leader = { has_role = general }
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = army_lad_interest_group_scope
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = army_lad_leader_scope
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { this = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
				NOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized }
				country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				relations:root >= relations_threshold:neutral
			}
			save_scope_as = fact_finding_mission_country_scope
			set_variable = fact_finding_mission_country_var
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_small
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sent_leader_on_military_mission_movement
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:army_lad_interest_group_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sent_leader_on_military_mission_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = revolution_pulse2_events.1.tt
			trigger_event = { id = revolution_pulse2_events.2 days = 365 popup = yes }
		}
		scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
			set_character_busy_and_immortal = yes
			set_variable = army_lad_leader_var
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.1.b
		scope:army_lad_interest_group_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_amassing_strength
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			80 = {
				scope:ongoing_revolution = {
					add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_small
				}
			}
			20 = {
				scope:ongoing_revolution = {
					add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_medium
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

revolution_pulse2_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = revolution_pulse2_events.2.t
	desc = revolution_pulse2_events.2.d
	flavor = revolution_pulse2_events.2.f

	category = revolution

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_steam_ship"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = fact_finding_mission_country_var
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_variable = army_lad_leader_var
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = fact_finding_mission_country_var
			}
			save_scope_as = fact_finding_mission_country_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = army_lad_leader_var
			}
			save_scope_as = army_lad_leader_scope
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = army_lad_interest_group_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				 any_civil_war = {
					civil_war_is_reactionary_revolution = yes
				}
			}
			random_civil_war = {
				limit = {
					civil_war_is_reactionary_revolution = yes
				}
				civil_war_save_scopes = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:army_lad_leader_scope = { set_character_busy_and_immortal = no }
		add_modifier = {
			name = results_of_the_fact_finding_mission
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				 exists = scope:ongoing_revolution
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					scope:ongoing_revolution = {
						add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_small
					}
				}
				50 = {
					scope:army_lad_interest_group_scope = {
						add_modifier = {
							name = ig_amassing_strength
							days = normal_modifier_time
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:army_lad_interest_group_scope = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = ig_amassing_strength
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
							NOT = { has_trait = innovative }
						}
					}
					scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
						add_trait = innovative
					}
				}
			}
			20 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
							NOT = { has_trait = meticulous }
						}
					}
					scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
						add_trait = meticulous
					}
				}
			}
			20 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
							NOT = { has_trait = charismatic }
						}
					}
					scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
						add_trait = charismatic
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
							NOT = { has_trait = syphilis }
						}
					}
					scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
						add_trait = syphilis
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
							NOT = { has_trait = cocaine_addiction }
						}
					}
					scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
						add_trait = cocaine_addiction
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
							NOT = { has_trait = expensive_tastes }
						}
					}
					scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
						add_trait = expensive_tastes
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
							NOT = { has_trait = bigoted }
						}
					}
					scope:army_lad_leader_scope = {
						add_trait = bigoted
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

revolution_pulse2_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	duration = 3

	title = revolution_pulse2_events.3.t
	desc = revolution_pulse2_events.3.d
	flavor = revolution_pulse2_events.3.f

	category = revolution

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_cops_march"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_civil_war = {
			OR = {
				civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
				civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
			}
			OR = {
				civil_war_progress_low = yes
				civil_war_progress_high = yes
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic }
		any_scope_state = {
			turmoil >= 0.20
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_industry_building = yes
					is_heavy_industry_building = yes
				}
				level >= 3
				occupancy >= 0.5
			}
			any_neighbouring_state = {
				owner = {
					country_rank >= root.country_rank
					OR = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
						has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
						NOT = {
							any_interest_group = {
								is_in_government = yes
								OR = {
									is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
									is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_civil_war = {
				OR = {
					civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
					civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
				}
				OR = {
					civil_war_progress_low = yes
					civil_war_progress_high = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_civil_war = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
					civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
				}
				OR = {
					civil_war_progress_low = yes
					civil_war_progress_high = yes
				}
			}
			civil_war_save_scopes = yes
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				turmoil >= 0.20
				any_scope_building = {
					OR = {
						is_industry_building = yes
						is_heavy_industry_building = yes
					}
					level >= 3
					occupancy >= 0.5
				}
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					owner = {
						country_rank >= root.country_rank
						OR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
							has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							NOT = {
								any_interest_group = {
									is_in_government = yes
									OR = {
										is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
										is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = transnational_unrest_squashing_state_scope
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_industry_building = yes
						is_heavy_industry_building = yes
					}
					level >= 3
					occupancy >= 0.5
				}
				save_scope_as = transnational_unrest_squashing_factory_scope
			}
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = {
						country_rank >= root.country_rank
						OR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
							has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							NOT = {
								any_interest_group = {
									is_in_government = yes
									OR = {
										is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
										is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				owner = {
					save_scope_as = transnational_unrest_squashing_country_scope
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = transnational_requested_foreign_intervention
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_medium
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = transnational_bloody_suppression
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.3.b
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_small
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = transnational_bloody_suppression
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.3.c
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				scope:ongoing_revolution = {
					add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_medium
				}
			}
			50 = {
				scope:ongoing_revolution = {
					add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_small
				}
			}
			25 = {
			}
		}
		scope:transnational_unrest_squashing_state_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = transnational_unrest_squashing_strike
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution_movement = {

			}

revolution_pulse2_events.4 = {
	type = country_event

	duration = 3

	title = revolution_pulse2_events.4.t
	desc = revolution_pulse2_events.4.d
	flavor = revolution_pulse2_events.4.f

	category = revolution

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_battlefield_trenches"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_variable = abolished_scrip_wages_var }
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic }
		any_civil_war = {
			OR = {
				civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
				civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
			}
			civil_war_progress_low = yes
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			turmoil >= 0.10
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_industry_building = yes
					is_heavy_industry_building = yes
					is_raw_industries_building = yes
				}
				level >= 3
				occupancy >= 0.5
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_civil_war = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
					civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
				}
				civil_war_progress_low = yes
			}
			civil_war_save_scopes = yes
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				turmoil >= 0.10
				any_scope_building = {
					OR = {
						is_industry_building = yes
						is_heavy_industry_building = yes
						is_raw_industries_building = yes
					}
					level >= 3
					occupancy >= 0.5
				}
			}
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_industry_building = yes
						is_heavy_industry_building = yes
						is_raw_industries_building = yes
					}
					level >= 3
					occupancy >= 0.5
				}
				save_scope_as = company_store_strike_building
			}
			save_scope_as = company_store_strike_state
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_civil_war = {
				OR = {
					civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
					civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
				}
				civil_war_progress_low = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:company_store_strike_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = laborers
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sided_with_capitalists_over_scrips
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				scope:ongoing_revolution_movement = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = transnational_bloody_suppression
						days = normal_modifier_time
						multiplier = 0.5
					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				scope:ongoing_revolution = {
					add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_small
				}
			}
			30 = {
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.4.b
		custom_tooltip = revolution_pulse2_events.4.tt
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = capitalists
			value = small_radicals
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sided_with_workers_over_scrips
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = abolished_scrip_wages
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		set_variable = abolished_scrip_wages_var
	}
}

revolution_pulse2_events.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:garrison_refusal_state

	duration = 3

	title = revolution_pulse2_events.5.t
	desc = revolution_pulse2_events.5.d
	flavor = revolution_pulse2_events.5.f

	category = revolution

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		any_civil_war = {
			OR = {
				civil_war_progress_medium = yes
				civil_war_progress_high = yes
			}
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_in_revolt = yes
			any_scope_building = {
			 	is_building_type = building_barrack
				level >= 3
				occupancy >= 0.3
			}
		}
		any_country = {
			has_diplomatic_pact = {
				who = root
     			type = rivalry
     		}
     		relations:root <= relations_threshold:cold
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_civil_war = {
				OR = {
					civil_war_progress_medium = yes
					civil_war_progress_high = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_civil_war = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					civil_war_progress_medium = yes
					civil_war_progress_high = yes
				}
			}
			civil_war_save_scopes = yes
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_in_revolt = yes
				any_scope_building = {
				 	is_building_type = building_barrack
					level >= 3
					occupancy >= 0.3
				}
			}
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
				 	is_building_type = building_barrack
					level >= 3
					occupancy >= 0.3
				}
				save_scope_as = garrison_refusal_barracks
			}
			save_scope_as = garrison_refusal_state
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
	     			type = rivalry
	     		}
	     		relations:root <= relations_threshold:cold
     		}
     		save_scope_as = garrison_refusal_rival
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:garrison_refusal_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = soldiers
				value = large_radicals
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = officers
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
		scope:garrison_refusal_state.state_region = {
			add_devastation = 10
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_small
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.5.b
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_small
		}
		scope:garrison_refusal_barracks = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = seditious_garrison
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_furious_at_cowardice
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

		any_civil_war = {
			OR = {
         		civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
         		civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
			}
			civil_war_progress_low = yes
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			OR = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
				is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
			}
			is_insurrectionary = yes
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes

		}
	}

					civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
				}
				civil_war_progress_low = yes

		}

					civil_war_is_socialist_revolution = yes
					civil_war_is_liberal_revolution = yes
				}
				civil_war_progress_low = yes
			}
			civil_war_save_scopes = yes
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				set_variable = custom_profession_hatmaker_var
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = custom_profession_watchmaker_var

			}

				set_variable = custom_profession_cooper_var
			}

				set_variable = custom_profession_smith_var
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = custom_profession_brewer_var
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = custom_profession_tailor_var
			}
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_insurrectionary = yes
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
					is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = hatmakerfeud_ig
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				turmoil >= 0.20
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			set_variable = hatmakerfeud_state_var
			save_scope_as = hatmakerfeud_state
		}
		set_variable = hatmakersfeud_var
	}

	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.7.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger_event = { id = revolution_pulse2_events.8 days = 7 popup = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.7.b
		scope:ongoing_revolution_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = hatmakersfeud_emboldened_commoners
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			80 = {
				create_character = {
					is_agitator = yes
					interest_group = scope:hatmakerfeud_ig
					ideology = ideology_radical
					traits = {
						inspirational_orator
						reckless
					}
				}
			}
			20 = {
			}
		}
	}
}

					is_building_type = building_barrack

					}

						add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_medium
					}
				}
				80 = {
					scope:ongoing_revolution = {
						add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_medium
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				40 = {
					scope:ongoing_revolution = {
						add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_medium
					}
				}
				60 = {
					scope:ongoing_revolution = {
						add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_medium
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:arms_depot_raided_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_scouring_countryside
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = revolution_pulse2_events.10.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:arms_depot_raided_state = {
					any_scope_building = {
						is_building_type = building_urban_center
						level > 10
					}
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				80 = {
					scope:ongoing_revolution = {

					}
				}
				20 = {
					scope:ongoing_revolution = {
						add_civil_war_progress = revolution_advance_medium
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {

						add_civil_war_progress = revolution_retreat_medium
					}
				}

﻿namespace = silkworm_diseases

# Pebrine Outbreak Start
silkworm_diseases.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:pebrine_state
	event_image = {
		video = "asia_dead_cattle_poor_harvest"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	#only fires once

	title = silkworm_diseases.1.t
	desc = silkworm_diseases.1.d
	flavor = silkworm_diseases.1.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
	            is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
	            level >= 5
	        }
		}
		OR = {
			can_research = pasteurization
			is_researching_technology = pasteurization
		}
		OR = {
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_europe
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_north_america
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = pebrine_outbreak_event
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = pebrine_outbreak_event
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_building = {
		            is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
		            level >= 5
		        }
			}
			save_scope_as = pebrine_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
		            level >= 5
				}
				save_scope_as = pebrine_building
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_silkworm_diseases
		}
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}


# Pebrine Outbreak Spread
silkworm_diseases.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:pebrine_state
	event_image = {
		video = "asia_dead_cattle_poor_harvest"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	title = silkworm_diseases.2.t

	desc = silkworm_diseases.2.d

	flavor = silkworm_diseases.2.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
	            is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
	            level >= 2
	        }
	        NOT = {
	        	has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
	        }
			NOT = {
				has_variable = var_has_had_pebrine
			}
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_building = {
		            is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
		            level >= 2
		        }
		        NOT = {
		        	has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
		        }
			}
			save_scope_as = pebrine_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
		            level >= 2
				}
				save_scope_as = pebrine_building
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.2.a
		trigger = {
			can_research = pasteurization
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_pebrine_research_funds
			days = short_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_medium
		}
		add_technology_progress = { progress = 4000 technology = pasteurization }
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
			set_variable = var_has_had_pebrine
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.2.b
		default_option = yes
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
			set_variable = var_has_had_pebrine
		}
	}
}

# Pebrine Outbreak Spread - International
silkworm_diseases.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:pebrine_state
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_trains"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	title = silkworm_diseases.3.t

	desc = silkworm_diseases.3.d

	flavor = silkworm_diseases.3.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		#Triggered by the JE
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = pebrine_outbreak_event
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_neighbouring_state = {
				is_incorporated = yes
					owner = {
						any_scope_state = {
							is_incorporated = yes
							has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					is_incorporated = yes
					owner = {
						any_scope_state = {
							is_incorporated = yes
							has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
						}
					}
				}
				owner = {
					save_scope_as = pebrine_origin
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
					market = ROOT.market
					any_scope_state = {
						has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
					market = ROOT.market
					any_scope_state = {
						has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = pebrine_origin
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
					has_journal_entry = je_silkworm_diseases
				}
				save_scope_as = pebrine_origin
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_building = {
		            is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
		            level >= 2
		        }
			}
			save_scope_as = pebrine_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
		            level >= 2
				}
				save_scope_as = pebrine_building
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_silkworm_diseases
		}
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.3.b
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_silkworm_diseases
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:pebrine_origin
			value = -10
		}
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Silkworm Farm Owners Demand Recompense
silkworm_diseases.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:pebrine_state
	event_image = {
		video = "asia_farmers_market"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	title = silkworm_diseases.4.t

	desc = silkworm_diseases.4.d

	flavor = silkworm_diseases.4.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		any_scope_state = {
			has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_tenant_farmers
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				has_building = building_silk_plantation
			}
			save_scope_as = pebrine_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
				}
				save_scope_as = pebrine_building
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = landowners_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.4.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_landowner_bailout_funds
			days = short_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_landowner_bailout_funds_country
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
				interest_group = scope:landowners_scope
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.4.b
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
				interest_group = scope:landowners_scope
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_landowners_not_bailed_out
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

silkworm_diseases.6 = {

		video = "asia_union_leader"
	}

	title = silkworm_diseases.6.t

	desc = silkworm_diseases.6.d

	flavor = silkworm_diseases.6.f

	duration = 3

	dlc = dlc004

	trigger = {
		any_scope_state = {
			has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				has_building = building_silk_plantation
			}
			save_scope_as = pebrine_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_silk_plantation
				}
				save_scope_as = pebrine_building
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.6.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_worker_bailout
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
				pop_type = farmers
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.6.b
		scope:pebrine_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = farmers
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_workers_not_bailed_out
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = silkworm_diseases.7.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				any_country = {
					has_variable = cure_sponsor_var
				}
			}
			any_country = {
				NOT = {
					THIS = ROOT
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
				}
				NOT = {
					has_variable = pebrine_cure
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pebrine_cure_acquire_tt
			set_variable = pebrine_cure
		}
		set_variable = cure_sponsor_var
		custom_tooltip = pebrine_foreign_aid_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						has_modifier = modifier_pebrine_state_modifier
					}
					NOT = {
						has_variable = pebrine_cure
					}
				}
				trigger_event = { id = silkworm_diseases.8 weeks = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

		}

		name = silkworm_diseases.8.a

		random_list = {
			25 = {
				set_owes_obligation_to = {
					country = scope:cure_sponsor_scope

				change_relations = {
					country = scope:cure_sponsor_scope
					value = 10
				}
			}

				change_relations = {
					country = scope:cure_sponsor_scope
					value = 10
				}
			}
		}
	}

		name = silkworm_diseases.8.b
		trigger = {
			scope:cure_sponsor_scope = {
				relations:root < 0
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:cure_sponsor_scope
			value = -10
		}
	}

﻿namespace = slave_revolt_events

# The Edict of Abolition (?)
slave_revolt_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_slave_chains"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	title = slave_revolt_events.1.t
	desc = slave_revolt_events.1.d
	flavor = slave_revolt_events.1.f

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by on_created
	}

	immediate = {
		random_primary_culture = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					this = cu:afro_caribbean
					this = cu:afro_antillean
					this = cu:afro_american
					this = cu:afro_caribeno
					this = cu:afro_brazilian
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = freed_slave_culture_scope
		}
	}

	option = { # Slavery is abolished
		name = slave_revolt_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		activate_law = law_type:law_slavery_banned
		add_loyalists = {
			value = small_radicals
			culture = scope:freed_slave_culture_scope
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 95
		}
	}
	option = { # The awful Dessalines-ish option. Slavery remains.
		name = slave_revolt_events.1.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_oligarchy
			}
		}
		change_infamy = 10
		add_radicals = {
			value = very_large_radicals
			culture = scope:freed_slave_culture_scope
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = yeet_agitator_events

# Infertile Grounds - Agitator is unpopular and cannot agitate
yeet_agitator_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_automobile"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	title = yeet_agitator_events.1.t
	desc = yeet_agitator_events.1.d
	flavor = yeet_agitator_events.1.f

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		any_interest_group = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_role = agitator
				can_agitate = root
				popularity <= -50
				NOT = { has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use }
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_1
			}
			scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_1 = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = agitator
					can_agitate = root
					popularity <= -50
					NOT = { has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use }
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_role = agitator
					can_agitate = root
					popularity <= -50
					NOT = { has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use }
				}
				save_scope_as = yeeted_agitator_scope_1
				set_variable = { name = temp_event_target_in_use }
			}
			save_scope_as = yeeted_agitator_ig_scope_1
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yeet_agitator_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = yeet_agitator_events.1.tt_1
			scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_1 = {
				hidden_effect = {
					remove_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
				}
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
		}
		scope:yeeted_agitator_ig_scope_1 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = embittered_writings_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = yeet_agitator_events.1.b
		scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_1 = {
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
			}
			exile_character = yes
		}
		scope:yeeted_agitator_ig_scope_1 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = agitator_exiled_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = yeet_agitator_events.1.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_1 = {
				popularity >= -10
			}
		}
		scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_1 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = yellow_press_adovacte_modifier
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = newspaper_campaign_modifier
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small
		}
	}
}

# To Greener Grounds - Agitator is popular and idle
yeet_agitator_events.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_garibaldi"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	title = yeet_agitator_events.2.t
	desc = yeet_agitator_events.2.d
	flavor = yeet_agitator_events.2.f

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = agitator
			NOT = {
				can_agitate = root
				has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
			}
			popularity >= 50
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_2
			}
			scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_2 = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = agitator
					NOT = {
						can_agitate = root
						has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
					}
					popularity >= 50
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_role = agitator
					NOT = {
						can_agitate = root
						has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
					}
					popularity >= 50
				}
				save_scope_as = yeeted_agitator_scope_2
				set_variable = { name = temp_event_target_in_use }
			}
			save_scope_as = yeeted_agitator_ig_scope_2
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yeet_agitator_events.2.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_2 = {
			exile_character = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = agitator_proof_modifier
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
	#option = {
	#	name = yeet_agitator_events.2.b
	#	random_list = {
	#		2 = {
	#			scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_2 = {
	#				radicalize_character = yes
	#			}
	#		}
	#		1 = {
	#			scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_2 = {
	#				exile_character = yes
	#			}
	#		}
	#	}
	#}
}

# The Fruits of Thy Labor - Agitator is old or not too popular
yeet_agitator_events.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_automobile"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	title = yeet_agitator_events.3.t
	desc = yeet_agitator_events.3.d
	flavor = yeet_agitator_events.3.f

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { months = 3 }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = agitator
			NOT = {
				can_agitate = root
				has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
			}
			OR = {
				popularity <= 49
				age >= 60
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_3
			}
			scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_3 = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = agitator
				NOT = {
					can_agitate = root
					has_variable = temp_event_target_in_use
				}
				OR = {
					popularity <= 49
					age >= 60
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = yeeted_agitator_scope_3
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = yeeted_agitator_ig_scope_3
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = retirement_state_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					is_heir = yes
					is_ruler = yes
					has_role = agitator
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = walter_scope
			set_variable = retirement_random_name_var
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_variable = retirement_random_name_var
					is_heir = yes
					is_ruler = yes
					has_role = agitator
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = donny_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yeet_agitator_events.3.a
	#	custom_tooltip = {
	#		text = yeet_agitator_events.3.tt
			hidden_effect = {
				scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_3 = {
					hidden_effect = { remove_variable = temp_event_target_in_use }
					kill_character = {
						hidden = yes
					}
				}
			}
	#	}
		scope:yeeted_agitator_ig_scope_3 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = agitator_legacy_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yeet_agitator_events.3.b
		default_option = yes
	#	custom_tooltip = {
	#		text = yeet_agitator_events.3.tt
			hidden_effect = {
				scope:yeeted_agitator_scope_3 = {
					hidden_effect = { remove_variable = temp_event_target_in_use }
					kill_character = {
						hidden = yes
					}
				}
			}
	#	}
		scope:yeeted_agitator_ig_scope_3 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = honouring_agitator_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}
